00074: function normalizeLotteryKey(value) {
00075:   return String(value || "PT_RIO")
00076:     .trim()
00077:     .toUpperCase() || "PT_RIO";
00078: }
00079: 
00080: function normalizeYmd(value) {
00081:   const text = String(value || "").trim();
00082: 
00083:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(text)) {
00084:     throw new Error(
00085:       "Data inválida. Utilize o formato YYYY-MM-DD."
00086:     );
00087:   }
00088: 
00089:   return text;
00090: }
00091: 
00092: function normalizeHour(value) {
00093:   const text = String(value || "")
00094:     .trim()
00095:     .replace(/[hH]/g, ":");
00096: 
00097:   const match = text.match(
00098:     /^(\d{1,2})(?::?(\d{2}))?$/
00099:   );
00100: 
00101:   if (!match) {
00102:     throw new Error(
00103:       "Horário inválido. Utilize HH:MM."
00104:     );
00105:   }
00106: 
00107:   const hour = Number(match[1]);
00108:   const minute = Number(match[2] || 0);
00109: 
00110:   if (
00111:     hour < 0 ||
00112:     hour > 23 ||
00113:     minute < 0 ||
00114:     minute > 59
00115:   ) {
00116:     throw new Error("Horário fora do intervalo válido.");
00117:   }
00118: 
00119:   return `${String(hour).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(
00120:     minute
00121:   ).padStart(2, "0")}`;
00122: }
00123: 
00124: function dateHourKey(ymd, hour) {
00125:   return `${ymd}T${normalizeHour(hour)}`;
00126: }
00127: 
00128: function subtractDaysYmd(ymd, days) {
00129:   const safeDays = Math.max(
00130:     1,
00131:     Math.min(1460, Number(days || 180))
00132:   );
00133: 
00134:   const date = new Date(`${ymd}T12:00:00Z`);
00135: 
00136:   if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
00137:     throw new Error(
00138:       "Não foi possível calcular a janela histórica."
00139:     );
00140:   }
00141: 
00142:   date.setUTCDate(
00143:     date.getUTCDate() - safeDays
00144:   );
00145: 
00146:   return date.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
00147: }

00149: function normalizeHistorySource(value) {

00151:     .trim()
00152:     .toLowerCase();

00154:   if (
00155:     source !== "auto" &&
00156:     source !== "snapshot" &&
00157:     source !== "range"

00159:     throw new Error(
00160:       "historySource inválido. Utilize auto, snapshot ou range."
00161:     );
00162:   }
00163: 
00164:   return source;
00165: }

00167: async function loadPredictionHistory({
00168:   lotteryKey,
00169:   date,
00170:   input = {},
00171:   dependencies = {},
00172: } = {}) {
00173:   const requestedSource = normalizeHistorySource(
00174:     input.historySource
00175:   );
00176: 
00177:   const loadMetadata =
00178:     dependencies.readHistoryMetadata ||
00179:     readMetadata;
00180: 
00181:   const loadFullHistory =
00182:     dependencies.readFullHistory ||
00183:     readFullHistory;
00184: 
00185:   const loadRangeHistory =
00186:     dependencies.fetchDraws ||
00187:     fetchDrawsWithPrizesByRange;
00188: 
00189:   if (
00190:     requestedSource === "auto" ||
00191:     requestedSource === "snapshot"
00192:   ) {
00193:     const metadataResult = await loadMetadata(
00194:       lotteryKey,
00195:       dependencies.historyDependencies || {}
00196:     );
00197: 
00198:     const metadata =
00199:       metadataResult?.data || null;
00200: 
00201:     const snapshotReady =
00202:       metadataResult?.exists === true &&
00203:       metadata?.bootstrapStatus === "complete";
00204: 
00205:     if (snapshotReady) {
00206:       const draws = await loadFullHistory(
00207:         lotteryKey,
00208:         dependencies.historyDependencies || {}
00209:       );
00210: 
00211:       if (Array.isArray(draws) && draws.length) {
00212:         const expectedTotal = Number(
00213:           metadata?.totalDraws || 0
00214:         );
00215: 
00216:         if (
00217:           expectedTotal > 0 &&
00218:           draws.length !== expectedTotal
00219:         ) {
00220:           throw new Error(
00221:             "Histórico TOP3 inconsistente: metadata.totalDraws=" +
00222:             `${expectedTotal}, carregados=${draws.length}.`
00223:           );
00224:         }
00225: 
00226:         return {
00227:           source: "snapshot",
00228:           draws,
00229:           metadata,
00230:           lookbackDays: null,
00231:           maxDraws: null,
00232:           startYmd:
00233:             metadata?.firstYmd || null,
00234:         };
00235:       }
00236: 
00237:       throw new Error(
00238:         "Metadata do histórico TOP3 está completo, mas nenhum draw foi carregado."
00239:       );
00240:     }
00241: 
00242:     if (requestedSource === "snapshot") {
00243:       throw new Error(
00244:         "Histórico TOP3 completo ainda não está disponível."
00245:       );
00246:     }
00247:   }
00248: 
00249:   const lookbackDays = Math.max(
00250:     30,
00251:     Math.min(
00252:       1460,
00253:       Number(input.lookbackDays || 180)
00254:     )
00255:   );
00256: 
00257:   const maxDraws = Math.max(
00258:     100,
00259:     Math.min(
00260:       5000,
00261:       Number(input.maxDraws || 1200)
00262:     )
00263:   );
00264: 
00265:   const startYmd = subtractDaysYmd(
00266:     date,
00267:     lookbackDays
00268:   );
00269: 
00270:   const rawDraws = await loadRangeHistory({
00271:     lottery: lotteryKey,
00272:     startYmd,
00273:     endYmd: date,
00274:     pageSize: Number(input.pageSize || 250),
00275:     maxDraws,
00276:     prizeConcurrency: Number(
00277:       input.prizeConcurrency || 24
00278:     ),
00279:   });
00280: 
00281:   return {
00282:     source: "range_fallback",
00283:     draws: extractDraws(rawDraws),
00284:     metadata: null,
00285:     lookbackDays,
00286:     maxDraws,
00287:     startYmd,
00288:   };
00289: }
00290: 
00291: function extractDraws(result) {
00292:   if (Array.isArray(result)) {
00293:     return result;
00294:   }
00295: 
00296:   const candidates = [
00297:     result?.draws,
00298:     result?.items,
00299:     result?.results,
00300:     result?.data,
00301:   ];
00302: 
00303:   for (const candidate of candidates) {
00304:     if (Array.isArray(candidate)) {
00305:       return candidate;
00306:     }
00307:   }
00308: 
00309:   return [];
00310: }

00312: function normalizePublicMilhar(value) {

00314:     .replace(/\D+/g, "");
00315: 
00316:   if (!digits) {
00317:     return "";

00328:     .slice(0, 2)
00329:     .padStart(2, "0");

00332: function resolveNextTop3Slot({
00333:   lotteryKey,
00334:   ymd,

00337: }) {

00361:   ).trim();
00362: 
00363:   const hourMatch =
00364:     rawHour.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?::00|h)?$/i);
00365: 
00366:   const canonicalHour = hourMatch
00367:     ? `${String(Number(hourMatch[1])).padStart(2, "0")}:00`
00368:     : "";
00369: 
00370:   return {
00371:     ...resolved,
00372:     ymd: normalizeYmd(resolved?.ymd),
00373:     hour: canonicalHour,
00374:     hourBucket: canonicalHour,
00375:   };
00376: }
00377: 
00378: function scheduleForPublicProjection(
00379:   lotteryKey,
00380:   date
00381: ) {
00382:   if (normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey) === "FEDERAL") {
00383:     return [...FEDERAL_SCHEDULE];
00384:   }
00385: 
00386:   const parsed = new Date(`${date}T12:00:00Z`);
00387:   const dow = parsed.getUTCDay();
00388: 
00389:   if (dow === 3 || dow === 6) {
00390:     return [...PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT];
00391:   }
00392: 
00393:   return [...PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL];
00394: }
00395: 
00396: function buildPublicMilharesCols(
00397:   engineOutput,
00398:   expectedCols = 4,
00399:   perCol = 5
00400: ) {
00401:   const dezenas = Array.isArray(engineOutput?.dezenas)
00402:     ? engineOutput.dezenas
00403:     : [];
00404: 
00405:   const slots = Array.isArray(engineOutput?.slots)
00406:     ? engineOutput.slots
00407:     : [];
00408: 
00409:   const cols = [];
00410: 
00411:   for (const dezena of dezenas.slice(0, expectedCols)) {
00412:     const items = slots
00413:       .filter(
00414:         (slot) =>
00415:           String(slot?.dezena || "") ===
00416:           String(dezena || "")
00417:       )
00418:       .map(
00419:         (slot) =>
00420:           normalizePublicMilhar(slot?.milhar)
00421:       )
00422:       .filter(
00423:         (milhar) => /^\d{4}$/.test(milhar)
00424:       )
00425:       .slice(0, perCol);
00426: 
00427:     while (items.length < perCol) {
00428:       items.push("");
00429:     }
00430: 
00431:     cols.push({
00432:       dezena: String(dezena || ""),
00433:       items,
00434:     });
00435:   }
00436: 
00437:   while (cols.length < expectedCols) {
00438:     cols.push({
00439:       dezena: "",
00440:       items: Array(perCol).fill(""),
00441:     });
00442:   }
00443: 
00444:   return cols.slice(0, expectedCols);
00445: }
00446: 
00447: function buildTop3PublicSnapshot({
00448:   computedTop,
00449:   history,
00450:   lotteryKey,
00451:   date,
00452:   closeHour,
00453:   publicApi,
00454: }) {
00455:   const schedule = scheduleForPublicProjection(
00456:     lotteryKey,
00457:     date
00458:   );
00459: 
00460:   return (Array.isArray(computedTop)
00461:     ? computedTop
00462:     : []
00463:   )
00464:     .slice(0, 3)
00465:     .map((item, index) => {
00466:       const grupo = Number(item?.grupo);
00467: 
00468:       if (
00469:         !Number.isFinite(grupo) ||
00470:         grupo < 1 ||
00471:         grupo > 25
00472:       ) {
00473:         return null;
00474:       }
00475: 
00476:       const probability = Number(
00477:         item?.scoreProb ??
00478:         item?.probability ??
00479:         item?.confidence ??
00480:         0
00481:       );
00482: 
00483:       const prob =
00484:         probability > 1
00485:           ? probability / 100
00486:           : probability;
00487: 
00488:       const engineOutput =
00489:         publicApi.build20MilharesForGrupo({
00490:           rangeDraws: history,
00491:           analysisHourBucket: closeHour,
00492:           schedule,
00493:           grupo2: grupo,
00494:           targetYmd: date,
00495:         });
00496: 
00497:       const milharesCols =
00498:         buildPublicMilharesCols(
00499:           engineOutput,
00500:           4,
00501:           5
00502:         );
00503: 
00504:       const milhares20 = milharesCols
00505:         .flatMap((column) => column.items)
00506:         .filter(
00507:           (milhar) => /^\d{4}$/.test(milhar)
00508:         )
00509:         .slice(0, 20);
00510: 
00511:       return {
00512:         rank: index + 1,
00513:         grupo,
00514:         animal:
00515:           item?.animal ||
00516:           ANIMALS[
00517:             String(grupo).padStart(2, "0")
00518:           ] ||
00519:           "",
00520:         prob:
00521:           Number.isFinite(prob)
00522:             ? prob
00523:             : 0,
00524:         probPct:
00525:           Number.isFinite(prob)
00526:             ? Number((prob * 100).toFixed(4))
00527:             : 0,
00528:         milhares20,
00529:         milharesCols,
00530:         meta: item?.meta || null,
00531:       };
00532:     })
00533:     .filter(Boolean);
00534: }
00535: 
00536: async function saveTop3PublicProjection({
00537:   lotteryKey,
00538:   date,
00539:   closeHour,
00540:   snapshot,
00541:   engineVersion = "V3_STATISTICAL",
00542:   source = "backend-top3",
00543: }) {
00544:   const lottery = normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey);
00545:   const hour = normalizeHour(closeHour);
00546:   const hourCode = publicHourCode(hour);
00547: 
00548:   const id =
00549:     `${lottery}__${date}__${hourCode}`;
00550: 
00551:   const normalizedSnapshot =
00552:     Array.isArray(snapshot)
00553:       ? snapshot.slice(0, 3)
00554:       : [];
00555: 
00556:   if (!normalizedSnapshot.length) {
00557:     throw new Error(
00558:       "Snapshot público TOP3 vazio."
00559:     );
00560:   }
00561: 
00562:   const picks = normalizedSnapshot
00563:     .map((item) => Number(item?.grupo))
00564:     .filter(
00565:       (grupo) =>
00566:         Number.isFinite(grupo) &&
00567:         grupo >= 1 &&
00568:         grupo <= 25
00569:     )
00570:     .slice(0, 3);
00571: 
00572:   if (!picks.length) {
00573:     throw new Error(
00574:       "Picks públicos TOP3 inválidos."
00575:     );
00576:   }
00577: 
00578:   const database = getDb();
00579:   const ref = database
00580:     .collection("top3_predictions")
00581:     .doc(id);
00582: 
00583:   const now = Date.now();
00584: 
00585:   const payload = {
00586:     id,
00587:     lotteryKey: lottery,
00588:     targetYmd: date,
00589:     targetHour:
00590:       `${hourCode}h`,
00591:     targetKey:
00592:       `${date}_${hourCode}h`,
00593:     picks,
00594:     snapshot: normalizedSnapshot,
00595:     engineVersion,
00596:     status: "predicted",
00597:     resultGrupo: null,
00598:     resultMilhar: "",
00599:     resultAnimal: "",
00600:     hitType: "",
00601:     hitScore: 0,
00602:     hitPosition: -1,
00603:     matchedValue: "",
00604:     createdAt: now,
00605:     updatedAt: now,
00606:     createdBy: source,
00607:     source,
00608:   };
00609: 
00610:   return database.runTransaction(
00611:     async (transaction) => {
00612:       const current =
00613:         await transaction.get(ref);
00614: 
00615:       if (!current.exists) {
00616:         transaction.set(ref, payload);
00617: 
00618:         return {
00619:           ok: true,
00620:           created: true,
00621:           updated: false,
00622:           existing: false,
00623:           protected: false,
00624:           id,
00625:         };
00626:       }
00627: 
00628:       const currentData =
00629:         current.data() || {};
00630: 
00631:       const currentStatus = String(
00632:         currentData.status || ""
00633:       )
00634:         .trim()
00635:         .toLowerCase();
00636: 
00637:       if (currentStatus === "validated") {
00638:         return {
00639:           ok: true,
00640:           created: false,
00641:           updated: false,
00642:           existing: true,
00643:           protected: true,
00644:           reason: "ALREADY_VALIDATED",
00645:           id,
00646:         };
00647:       }
00648: 
00649:       const updatedPayload = {
00650:         ...payload,
00651:         createdAt:
00652:           currentData.createdAt ||
00653:           payload.createdAt,
00654:         updatedAt: Date.now(),
00655:         resultGrupo:
00656:           currentData.resultGrupo ?? null,
00657:         resultMilhar:
00658:           currentData.resultMilhar || "",
00659:         resultAnimal:
00660:           currentData.resultAnimal || "",
00661:         hitType:
00662:           currentData.hitType || "",
00663:         hitScore:
00664:           Number(currentData.hitScore || 0),
00665:         hitPosition:
00666:           Number.isFinite(
00667:             Number(currentData.hitPosition)
00668:           )
00669:             ? Number(currentData.hitPosition)
00670:             : -1,
00671:         matchedValue:
00672:           currentData.matchedValue || "",
00673:       };
00674: 
00675:       transaction.set(
00676:         ref,
00677:         updatedPayload,
00678:         { merge: true }
00679:       );
00680: 
00681:       return {
00682:         ok: true,
00683:         created: false,
00684:         updated: true,
00685:         existing: true,
00686:         protected: false,
00687:         id,
00688:       };
00689:     }
00690:   );
00691: }
00692: 
00693: function mapTop3ToPredictions(top = []) {
00694:   return (Array.isArray(top) ? top : [])
00695:     .slice(0, 3)
00696:     .map((item, index) => {
00697:       const grupoNumber = Number(item?.grupo);
00698:       const grupo = String(grupoNumber).padStart(2, "0");
00699: 
00700:       const probability = Number(
00701:         item?.scoreProb ??
00702:         item?.probability ??
00703:         item?.confidence ??
00704:         0
00705:       );
00706: 
00707:       const percentage =
00708:         probability > 0 && probability <= 1
00709:           ? probability * 100
00710:           : probability;
00711: 
00712:       return {
00713:         type: "grupo",
00714:         grupo,
00715:         animal:
00716:           item?.animal ||
00717:           ANIMALS[grupo] ||
00718:           null,
00719:         score: Number(
00720:           Number.isFinite(percentage)
00721:             ? percentage.toFixed(4)
00722:             : 0
00723:         ),
00724:         confidence: Number(
00725:           Number.isFinite(percentage)
00726:             ? percentage.toFixed(4)
00727:             : 0
00728:         ),
00729:         reasons: Array.isArray(item?.reasons)
00730:           ? item.reasons.filter(Boolean)
00731:           : [],
00732:         signals: {
00733:           engine: "top3_statistical_v3",
00734:           rankPosition: index + 1,
00735:           scoreProb: Number(item?.scoreProb || 0),
00736:           rawScore: Number(item?.score || 0),
00737:           meta: item?.meta || null,
00738:         },
00739:       };
00740:     })
00741:     .filter((item) => /^\d{2}$/.test(item.grupo));
00742: }
00743: 
00744: async function createTop3PredictionRun(
00745:   input = {},
00746:   dependencies = {}
00747: ) {
00748:   const lotteryKey = normalizeLotteryKey(
00749:     input.lotteryKey
00750:   );
00751: 
00752:   const date = normalizeYmd(input.date);
00753:   const closeHour = normalizeHour(input.closeHour);
00754: 
00755:   const computeTop3 =
00756:     dependencies.computeTop3 ||
00757:     computeStatisticalTop3V3;
00758: 
00759:   const persistRun =
00760:     dependencies.persistRun ||
00761:     createPredictionRun;
00762: 
00763:   const publicApi =
00764:     dependencies.publicApi ||
00765:     loadTop3PublicApi();
00766: 
00767:   const historyLoad = await loadPredictionHistory({
00768:     lotteryKey,
00769:     date,
00770:     input,
00771:     dependencies,
00772:   });
00773: 
00774:   const allDraws = extractDraws(
00775:     historyLoad.draws
00776:   );
00777: 
00778:   const {
00779:     source: historySource,
00780:     metadata: historyMetadata,
00781:     lookbackDays,
00782:     maxDraws,
00783:     startYmd,
00784:   } = historyLoad;
00785: 
00786:   if (!allDraws.length) {
00787:     throw new Error(
00788:       `Nenhum resultado encontrado para ${lotteryKey}.`
00789:     );
00790:   }
00791: 
00792:   const targetKey = dateHourKey(date, closeHour);
00793: 
00794:   const history = allDraws
00795:     .map((draw) => {
00796:       const ymd =
00797:         publicApi.pickDrawYMD(draw);
00798: 
00799:       const rawHour =
00800:         publicApi.pickDrawHour(draw);
00801: 
00802:       if (!ymd || !rawHour) {
00803:         return null;
00804:       }
00805: 
00806:       const hour =
00807:         normalizeHour(rawHour);
00808: 
00809:       return {
00810:         draw,
00811:         ymd,
00812:         hour,
00813:         key: `${ymd}T${hour}`,
00814:       };
00815:     })
00816:     .filter(
00817:       (item) =>
00818:         item &&
00819:         item.key < targetKey
00820:     )
00821:     .sort((a, b) =>
00822:       a.key.localeCompare(b.key)
00823:     )
00824:     .map((item) => item.draw);
00825: 
00826:   if (!history.length) {
00827:     throw new Error(
00828:       "Não existe histórico anterior ao horário solicitado."
00829:     );
00830:   }
00831: 
00832:   const drawLast = history[history.length - 1];
00833: 
00834:   const drawsToday = history.filter(
00835:     (draw) => publicApi.pickDrawYMD(draw) === date
00836:   );
00837: 
00838:   const computed = computeTop3({
00839:     lotteryKey,
00840:     drawsRange: history,
00841:     drawLast,
00842:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00843:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
00844:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
00845:     topN: 3,
00846:     targetYmdOverride: date,
00847:     targetHourOverride: closeHour,
00848:     drawsAlreadySorted: true,
00849:   });
00850: 
00851:   const predictions = mapTop3ToPredictions(
00852:     computed?.top
00853:   );
00854: 
00855:   const publicSnapshot =
00856:     buildTop3PublicSnapshot({
00857:       computedTop: computed?.top,
00858:       history,
00859:       lotteryKey,
00860:       date,
00861:       closeHour,
00862:       publicApi,
00863:     });
00864: 
00865:   if (!predictions.length) {
00866:     throw new Error(
00867:       "O motor TOP3 não produziu previsões válidas."
00868:     );
00869:   }
00870: 
00871:   if (
00872:     publicSnapshot.length !== 3 ||
00873:     publicSnapshot.some(
00874:       (item) =>
00875:         !Array.isArray(item?.milhares20) ||
00876:         item.milhares20.length !== 20
00877:     )
00878:   ) {
00879:     throw new Error(
00880:       "O motor TOP3 não produziu 20 milhares válidas para cada grupo."
00881:     );
00882:   }
00883: 
00884:   const metadata = {
00885:     ...(input.metadata || {}),
00886:     engine: "top3_statistical_v3",
00887:     historyDraws: history.length,
00888:     drawsToday: drawsToday.length,
00889:     historySource,
00890:     historyBootstrapStatus:
00891:       historyMetadata?.bootstrapStatus || null,
00892:     historyTotalStored:
00893:       Number(historyMetadata?.totalDraws || 0) || null,
00894:     lookbackDays,
00895:     maxDraws,
00896:     startYmd,
00897:     lastDrawYmd:
00898:       publicApi.pickDrawYMD(drawLast) || null,
00899:     lastDrawHour:
00900:       publicApi.pickDrawHour(drawLast) || null,
00901:     targetYmd: date,
00902:     targetHour: closeHour,
00903:     engineMeta: computed?.meta || null,
00904:   };
00905: 
00906:   const engine = {
00907:     name: "top3_statistical_v3",
00908:     historyDraws: history.length,
00909:     drawsToday: drawsToday.length,
00910:     historySource,
00911:     historyBootstrapStatus:
00912:       historyMetadata?.bootstrapStatus || null,
00913:     historyTotalStored:
00914:       Number(historyMetadata?.totalDraws || 0) || null,
00915:     lookbackDays,
00916:     maxDraws,
00917:     startYmd,
00918:     targetYmd: date,
00919:     targetHour: closeHour,
00920:     meta: computed?.meta || null,
00921:   };
00922: 
00923:   if (input.dryRun === true) {
00924:     return {
00925:       run: null,
00926:       predictions,
00927:       publicSnapshot,
00928:       engine,
00929:       dryRun: true,
00930:     };
00931:   }
00932: 
00933:   const source =
00934:     input.source || "backend-top3";
00935: 
00936:   const result = await persistRun({
00937:     lotteryKey,
00938:     date,
00939:     closeHour,
00940:     source,
00941:     algorithm: "top3_statistical_v3",
00942:     metadata,
00943:     predictions,
00944:   });
00945: 
00946:   const publicProjection =
00947:     await saveTop3PublicProjection({
00948:       lotteryKey,
00949:       date,
00950:       closeHour,
00951:       snapshot: publicSnapshot,
00952:       engineVersion: "V3_STATISTICAL",
00953:       source,
00954:     });
00955: 
00956:   return {
00957:     ...result,
00958:     publicSnapshot,
00959:     publicProjection,
00960:     engine,
00961:     dryRun: false,
00962:   };
00963: }
00964: 
00965: module.exports = {
00966:   createTop3PredictionRun,
00967:   mapTop3ToPredictions,
00968:   normalizeHour,
00969:   normalizeHistorySource,
00970:   loadPredictionHistory,
00971:   buildPublicMilharesCols,
00972:   buildTop3PublicSnapshot,
00973:   saveTop3PublicProjection,
00974:   resolveNextTop3Slot,
00975: };

00001: "use strict";
00002: 
00003: const { fetchAllDrawsWithPrizes } = require("./drawRepository");
00004: const { writeStatisticsSnapshot } = require("./statisticsEngine");
00005: 
00006: // Ajuste estes requires caso os arquivos estejam em outro caminho
00007: const { buildRanking } = require("../../src/utils/buildRanking");
00008: const { applyScoreEngine } = require("../../src/utils/scoreEngine");
00009: 
00010: async function bootstrapSnapshot(lottery = "PT_RIO") {
00011: 
00012:   const draws = await fetchAllDrawsWithPrizes({
00013:     lottery,
00014:     pageSize: 500,
00015:   });
00016: 
00017:   console.log("Draws carregados:", draws.length);
00018: 
00019:   const built = buildRanking(draws);
00020: 
00021:   const ranking = Array.isArray(built?.ranking)
00022:     ? built.ranking
00023:     : [];
00024: 
00025:   const rankingScored = applyScoreEngine(ranking);
00026: 
00027:   await writeStatisticsSnapshot({
00028:     uf: lottery,
00029:     scope: "dashboard_full",
00030:     data: {
00031:       version: 1,
00032:       totalDraws: built.totalDraws,
00033:       uniqueDays: built.uniqueDays,
00034:       totalOcorrencias: built.totalOcorrencias,
00035:       countMode: built.countMode,
00036:       ranking,
00037:       rankingScored,
00038:       top3: rankingScored.slice(0,3),
00039:       integrityIssues: built.integrityIssues || [],
00040:       generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
00041:     },
00042:   });
00043: 
00044:   return {
00045:     draws: draws.length,
00046:     ranking: ranking.length,
00047:     top3: rankingScored.slice(0,3),
00048:   };
00049: }
00050: 
00051: module.exports = {
00052:   bootstrapSnapshot,
00053: };

00001: "use strict";
00002: 
00003: /**
00004:  * Context Evidence V2
00005:  *
00006:  * Responsabilidade:
00007:  * Produzir evidências do contexto do sorteio.
00008:  * Não calcula score.
00009:  */
00010: 
00011: function normalize(value) {
00012:     const n = Number(value);
00013:     return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
00014: }
00015: 
00016: function buildReasons(ctx) {
00017: 
00018:     const reasons = [];
00019: 
00020:     reasons.push("Contexto histórico identificado.");
00021: 
00022:     if (ctx.lotteryKey)
00023:         reasons.push(`Loteria: ${ctx.lotteryKey}`);
00024: 
00025:     if (ctx.weekday)
00026:         reasons.push(`Dia da semana: ${ctx.weekday}`);
00027: 
00028:     if (ctx.closeHour)
00029:         reasons.push(`Horário: ${ctx.closeHour}`);
00030: 
00031:     if (ctx.previousGroup != null)
00032:         reasons.push(`Grupo anterior: ${ctx.previousGroup}`);
00033: 
00034:     if (ctx.previousAnimal)
00035:         reasons.push(`Animal anterior: ${ctx.previousAnimal}`);
00036: 
00037:     return reasons;
00038: }
00039: 
00040: function buildContextEvidence(item = {}, context = {}) {
00041: 
00042:     const evidence = {
00043: 
00044:         lotteryKey: context.lotteryKey || null,
00045: 
00046:         ymd: context.ymd || null,
00047: 
00048:         weekday: context.weekday || null,
00049: 
00050:         closeHour: context.hour || null,
00051: 
00052:         prize: normalize(context.prize),
00053: 
00054:         previousAnimal: context.previousAnimal || null,
00055: 
00056:         previousGroup: normalize(context.previousGroup),
00057: 
00058:         previousHour: context.previousHour || null,
00059: 
00060:         lookback: normalize(context.lookback),
00061: 
00062:         window: normalize(context.window),
00063: 
00064:     };
00065: 
00066:     return {
00067: 
00068:         module: "context",
00069: 
00070:         value: 1,
00071: 
00072:         evidence,
00073: 
00074:         reasons: buildReasons(evidence),
00075: 
00076:     };
00077: 
00078: }
00079: 
00080: module.exports = {
00081: 
00082:     buildContextEvidence,
00083: 
00084: };

00001: "use strict";
00002: 
00003: const { getAdminDb } = require("./statisticsEngine");
00004: 
00005: function normalizeLottery(lottery) {
00006:   return String(lottery || "PT_RIO").trim().toUpperCase();
00007: }
00008: 
00009: function normalizeGrupo(v) {
00010:   const n = Number(v);
00011:   return Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 1 && n <= 25 ? n : null;
00012: }
00013: 
00014: function normalizePosition(v) {
00015:   const n = Number(v);
00016:   return Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 1 && n <= 10 ? n : null;
00017: }
00018: 
00019: async function fetchPrizesForDrawRef(drawRef) {
00020:   const snap = await drawRef.collection("prizes").get();
00021: 
00022:   return snap.docs
00023:     .map((doc) => {
00024:       const data = doc.data() || {};
00025:       return {
00026:         id: doc.id,
00027:         ...data,
00028:         grupo: normalizeGrupo(data.grupo),
00029:         position: normalizePosition(data.position),
00030:       };
00031:     })
00032:     .filter((p) => p.grupo && p.position);
00033: }
00034: 
00035: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, concurrency, mapper) {
00036:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
00037:   const out = new Array(arr.length);
00038:   let index = 0;
00039: 
00040:   async function worker() {
00041:     while (index < arr.length) {
00042:       const i = index++;
00043:       out[i] = await mapper(arr[i], i);
00044:     }
00045:   }
00046: 
00047:   await Promise.all(
00048:     Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, worker)
00049:   );
00050: 
00051:   return out;
00052: }
00053: 
00054: 
00055: async function fetchDrawsWithPrizesByRange({
00056:   lottery = "PT_RIO",
00057:   startYmd,
00058:   endYmd,
00059:   pageSize = 250,
00060:   maxDraws = 1200,
00061:   prizeConcurrency = 24,
00062: } = {}) {
00063:   const db = getAdminDb();
00064:   const lk = normalizeLottery(lottery);
00065: 
00066:   const start = String(startYmd || "").trim();
00067:   const end = String(endYmd || "").trim();
00068: 
00069:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(start)) {
00070:     throw new Error(
00071:       "fetchDrawsWithPrizesByRange exige startYmd em YYYY-MM-DD."
00072:     );
00073:   }
00074: 
00075:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(end)) {
00076:     throw new Error(
00077:       "fetchDrawsWithPrizesByRange exige endYmd em YYYY-MM-DD."
00078:     );
00079:   }
00080: 
00081:   const safePageSize = Math.max(
00082:     25,
00083:     Math.min(500, Number(pageSize || 250))
00084:   );
00085: 
00086:   const safeMaxDraws = Math.max(
00087:     100,
00088:     Math.min(5000, Number(maxDraws || 1200))
00089:   );
00090: 
00091:   const safeConcurrency = Math.max(
00092:     1,
00093:     Math.min(50, Number(prizeConcurrency || 24))
00094:   );
00095: 
00096:   const admin = require("firebase-admin");
00097:   const DOC_ID = admin.firestore.FieldPath.documentId();
00098: 
00099:   const draws = [];
00100:   let lastDoc = null;
00101:   let page = 0;
00102: 
00103:   while (draws.length < safeMaxDraws) {
00104:     page += 1;
00105: 
00106:     const remaining = safeMaxDraws - draws.length;
00107:     const currentLimit = Math.min(
00108:       safePageSize,
00109:       remaining
00110:     );
00111: 
00112:     let q = db
00113:       .collection("draws")
00114:       .where("lottery_key", "==", lk)
00115:       .where("ymd", ">=", start)
00116:       .where("ymd", "<=", end)
00117:       .orderBy("ymd", "asc")
00118:       .orderBy(DOC_ID, "asc")
00119:       .limit(currentLimit);
00120: 
00121:     if (lastDoc) {
00122:       q = q.startAfter(lastDoc);
00123:     }
00124: 
00125:     console.log(
00126:       `[TOP3] Página ${page} | ${start} até ${end} | limite=${currentLimit}`
00127:     );
00128: 
00129:     const snap = await q.get();
00130: 
00131:     if (snap.empty) {
00132:       break;
00133:     }
00134: 
00135:     const pageDraws = await mapWithConcurrency(
00136:       snap.docs,
00137:       safeConcurrency,
00138:       async (doc) => {
00139:         const data = doc.data() || {};
00140:         const prizes = await fetchPrizesForDrawRef(
00141:           doc.ref
00142:         );
00143: 
00144:         return {
00145:           id: doc.id,
00146:           drawId: doc.id,
00147:           ...data,
00148:           prizes,
00149:         };
00150:       }
00151:     );
00152: 
00153:     draws.push(...pageDraws);
00154: 
00155:     console.log(
00156:       `[TOP3] Página ${page}: ${snap.docs.length} draws | total=${draws.length}`
00157:     );
00158: 
00159:     lastDoc = snap.docs[snap.docs.length - 1];
00160: 
00161:     if (snap.docs.length < currentLimit) {
00162:       break;
00163:     }
00164:   }
00165: 
00166:   return draws;
00167: }

00001: "use strict";
00002: 
00003: const { getDb } = require("../service/firebaseAdmin");
00004: 
00005: function normalizeWeekday(value) {
00006:   if (value == null) return null;
00007: 
00008:   const map = {
00009:     domingo:0,
00010:     segunda:1,
00011:     terca:2,
00012:     terça:2,
00013:     quarta:3,
00014:     quinta:4,
00015:     sexta:5,
00016:     sabado:6,
00017:     sábado:6,
00018:   };
00019: 
00020:   const s = String(value).trim().toLowerCase();
00021:   return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(map, s) ? map[s] : null;
00022: }
00023: 
00024: function getWeekday(ymd) {
00025:   const d = new Date(`${ymd}T12:00:00-03:00`);
00026:   return d.getDay();
00027: }
00028: 
00029: /**
00030:  * Query Engine
00031:  *
00032:  * Futuras otimizações:
00033:  * - cache por consulta
00034:  * - paginação
00035:  * - consultas paralelas
00036:  * - filtros compostos
00037:  */
00038: async function queryOccurrences({
00039:   lotteryKey,
00040:   entityType,
00041:   entityValue,
00042:   filters = {},
00043:   limitSamples = 20,
00044: } = {}) {
00045: 
00046:   if (!lotteryKey) throw new Error("lotteryKey obrigatório");
00047:   if (!entityType) throw new Error("entityType obrigatório");
00048: 
00049:   const db = getDb();
00050: 
00051:   let q = db.collection("draws")
00052:     .where("lottery_key","==",lotteryKey);
00053: 
00054:   if (filters.closeHour) {
00055:     q = q.where("close_hour","==",filters.closeHour);
00056:   }
00057: 
00058:   const draws = await q.get();
00059: 
00060:   const cache = {
00061:     draws: draws.size,
00062:     analyzed: 0,
00063:     matched: 0,
00064:   };
00065: 
00066: 
00067:   const wantedWeekday = normalizeWeekday(filters.weekday);
00068: 
00069:   let drawsAnalyzed = 0;
00070:   let occurrences = 0;
00071:   const matches = [];
00072: 
00073:   for (const draw of draws.docs) {
00074: 
00075:     const data = draw.data();
00076: 
00077:     if (wantedWeekday !== null) {
00078:       if (getWeekday(data.ymd) !== wantedWeekday) {
00079:         continue;
00080:       }
00081:     }
00082: 
00083:     drawsAnalyzed++;
00084:     cache.analyzed++;
00085: 
00086: 
00087:     let prizesQuery = draw.ref.collection("prizes");
00088: 
00089:     if (filters.position != null) {
00090:       prizesQuery = prizesQuery.where("position","==",Number(filters.position));
00091:     }
00092: 
00093:     const prizes = await prizesQuery.get();
00094: 
00095:     for (const p of prizes.docs) {
00096: 
00097:       const prize = p.data();
00098: 
00099:       const ok =
00100:         String(prize[entityType] ?? "")
00101:           .toLowerCase()
00102:           ==
00103:         String(entityValue)
00104:           .toLowerCase();
00105: 
00106:       if (!ok) continue;
00107: 
00108:       occurrences++;
00109:       cache.matched++;
00110: 
00111: 
00112:       if (matches.length < limitSamples) {
00113: 
00114:         matches.push({
00115: 
00116:           drawId: draw.id,
00117: 
00118:           ymd: data.ymd,
00119: 
00120:           closeHour: data.close_hour,
00121: 
00122:           lotteryKey: data.lottery_key,
00123: 
00124:           position: prize.position,
00125: 
00126:           animal: prize.animal,
00127: 
00128:           grupo: prize.grupo,
00129: 
00130:           dezena: prize.dezena,
00131: 
00132:           centena: prize.centena,
00133: 
00134:           milhar: prize.milhar,
00135: 
00136:         });
00137: 
00138:       }
00139: 
00140:     }
00141: 
00142:   }
00143: 
00144:   return {
00145: 
00146:     cache,
00147: 
00148:     statistics: {
00149: 
00150:       drawsAnalyzed,
00151: 
00152:       occurrences,
00153: 
00154:       percentage:
00155:         drawsAnalyzed
00156:           ? Number((occurrences*100/drawsAnalyzed).toFixed(2))
00157:           : 0
00158: 
00159:     },
00160: 
00161:     matches
00162: 
00163:   };
00164: 
00165: }
00166: 
00167: module.exports = {
00168: 
00169:   queryOccurrences,
00170: 
00171: };

00022:   const h = String(closeHour || "").trim().replace(":", "");

00016: function normalizeHour(value) {
00017:   const raw = String(
00018:     value ?? ""
00019:   ).trim();
00020: 
00021:   if (!raw) {
00022:     return "";
00023:   }

00026:     /^(\d{1,2})(?::?(\d{2}))?/
00027:   );

00030:     return "";

00035:     match[2] || 0
00036:   );

00039:     !Number.isFinite(hour) ||

00042:     !Number.isFinite(minute) ||

00050:     String(hour).padStart(2, "0") +
00051:     ":" +
00052:     String(minute).padStart(2, "0")
00053:   );

00250: function collectSameHourFrequency(

00281:       pickFirstPrizeGroup(draw);
00282: 
00283:     if (group == null) {
00284:       continue;
00285:     }
00286: 
00287:     samples += 1;
00288: 
00289:     counts.set(
00290:       group,
00291:       Number(
00292:         counts.get(group) || 0
00293:       ) + 1
00294:     );
00295:   }
00296: 
00297:   return {
00298:     counts,
00299:     samples,
00300:   };
00301: }
00302: 
00303: function computeProbability(
00304:   count,
00305:   samples
00306: ) {
00307:   const denominator =
00308:     Number(samples || 0) +
00309:     (
00310:       LAPLACE_ALPHA *
00311:       GROUPS_K
00312:     );
00313: 
00314:   return denominator > 0
00315:     ? (
00316:         Number(count || 0) +
00317:         LAPLACE_ALPHA
00318:       ) / denominator
00319:     : 1 / GROUPS_K;
00320: }
00321: 
00322: function sameGroupSet(
00323:   first,
00324:   second
00325: ) {
00326:   const a = safeArray(first)
00327:     .map(extractCandidateGroup)
00328:     .filter(Number.isFinite)
00329:     .sort(
00330:       (x, y) => x - y
00331:     );
00332: 
00333:   const b = safeArray(second)
00334:     .map(extractCandidateGroup)
00335:     .filter(Number.isFinite)
00336:     .sort(
00337:       (x, y) => x - y
00338:     );
00339: 
00340:   return (
00341:     a.length === b.length &&
00342:     a.every(
00343:       (value, index) =>
00344:         value === b[index]
00345:     )
00346:   );
00347: }
00348: 
00349: function computeStatisticalTop3E08Experimental(
00350:   input = {}
00351: ) {
00352:   const baseline =
00353:     computeStatisticalTop3V3(
00354:       input
00355:     );
00356: 
00357:   const baselineTop =
00358:     safeArray(baseline?.top)
00359:       .slice(0, 3);
00360: 
00361:   if (baselineTop.length < 3) {
00362:     return {
00363:       ...baseline,
00364:       experimental: {
00365:         enabled: true,
00366:         experiment: "E08",
00367:         applied: false,
00368:         reason:
00369:           "BASELINE_WITH_LESS_THAN_3",
00370:       },
00371:     };
00372:   }
00373: 
00374:   const targetHour =
00375:     normalizeHour(
00376:       input.targetHourOverride
00377:     );
00378: 
00379:   const frequency =
00380:     collectSameHourFrequency(
00381:       input.drawsRange,
00382:       targetHour
00383:     );
00384: 
00385:   const rerankedTop =
00386:     baselineTop
00387:       .map(
00388:         (item, index) => {
00389:           const group =
00390:             extractCandidateGroup(
00391:               item
00392:             );
00393: 
00394:           const count = Number(
00395:             frequency.counts.get(
00396:               group
00397:             ) || 0
00398:           );
00399: 
00400:           const sameHourProbability =
00401:             computeProbability(
00402:               count,
00403:               frequency.samples
00404:             );
00405: 
00406:           return {
00407:             ...item,
00408:             __baselineRank:
00409:               index + 1,
00410:             __sameHourCount:
00411:               count,
00412:             __sameHourProbability:
00413:               sameHourProbability,
00414:           };
00415:         }
00416:       )
00417:       .sort((a, b) => {
00418:         if (
00419:           b.__sameHourProbability !==
00420:           a.__sameHourProbability
00421:         ) {
00422:           return (
00423:             b.__sameHourProbability -
00424:             a.__sameHourProbability
00425:           );
00426:         }
00427: 
00428:         return (
00429:           a.__baselineRank -
00430:           b.__baselineRank
00431:         );
00432:       })
00433:       .map(
00434:         (item, index) => {
00435:           const {
00436:             __baselineRank,
00437:             __sameHourCount,
00438:             __sameHourProbability,
00439:             ...original
00440:           } = item;
00441: 
00442:           return {
00443:             ...original,
00444:             rank: index + 1,
00445:             title:
00446:               index === 0
00447:                 ? "Mais provável"
00448:                 : index === 1
00449:                   ? "2º mais provável"
00450:                   : "3º mais provável",
00451:             meta: {
00452:               ...(original?.meta || {}),
00453:               experiment: "E08",
00454:               baselineRank:
00455:                 __baselineRank,
00456:               sameHourCount:
00457:                 __sameHourCount,
00458:               sameHourProbability:
00459:                 __sameHourProbability,
00460:               sameHourSamples:
00461:                 frequency.samples,
00462:               targetHour,
00463:             },
00464:           };
00465:         }
00466:       );
00467: 
00468:   if (
00469:     !sameGroupSet(
00470:       baselineTop,
00471:       rerankedTop
00472:     )
00473:   ) {
00474:     throw new Error(
00475:       "E08 alterou o conjunto de candidatos da V3."
00476:     );
00477:   }
00478: 
00479:   return {
00480:     ...baseline,
00481:     top: rerankedTop,
00482:     ranking: rerankedTop,
00483:     baselineTop,
00484:     experimental: {
00485:       enabled: true,
00486:       experiment: "E08",
00487:       version: 1,
00488:       applied: true,
00489:       strategy:
00490:         "RERANK_V3_TOP3_BY_SAME_HOUR_FREQUENCY",
00491:       preservesCandidateSet: true,
00492:       targetHour,
00493:       samples:
00494:         frequency.samples,
00495:       baselineGroups:
00496:         baselineTop.map(
00497:           extractCandidateGroup
00498:         ),
00499:       rerankedGroups:
00500:         rerankedTop.map(
00501:           extractCandidateGroup
00502:         ),
00503:     },
00504:     meta: {
00505:       ...(baseline?.meta || {}),
00506:       scenario:
00507:         "E08_V3_TOP3_RERANK",
00508:       experimental: {
00509:         experiment: "E08",
00510:         preservesCandidateSet:
00511:           true,
00512:         targetHour,
00513:         samples:
00514:           frequency.samples,
00515:       },
00516:     },
00517:   };
00518: }
00519: 
00520: module.exports = {
00521:   collectSameHourFrequency,
00522:   computeStatisticalTop3E08Experimental,
00523: };

00007: const GROUPS_K = 25;

00010: function safeArray(value) {
00011:   return Array.isArray(value) ? value : [];
00012: }
00013: 
00014: function normalizeHour(value) {
00015:   const raw = String(value ?? "").trim();
00016: 
00017:   if (!raw) {
00018:     return "";
00019:   }
00020: 
00021:   const match = raw.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?::?(\d{2}))?/);
00022: 
00023:   if (!match) {
00024:     return "";
00025:   }
00026: 
00027:   const hour = Number(match[1]);
00028:   const minute = Number(match[2] || 0);
00029: 
00030:   if (
00031:     !Number.isFinite(hour) ||
00032:     hour < 0 ||
00033:     hour > 23 ||
00034:     !Number.isFinite(minute) ||
00035:     minute < 0 ||
00036:     minute > 59
00037:   ) {
00038:     return "";
00039:   }
00040: 
00041:   return `${String(hour).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(minute).padStart(2, "0")}`;
00042: }
00043: 
00044: function normalizeYmd(value) {
00045:   const raw = String(value ?? "").trim();
00046: 
00047:   const isoMatch = raw.match(
00048:     /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/
00049:   );
00050: 
00051:   if (isoMatch) {
00052:     return `${isoMatch[1]}-${isoMatch[2]}-${isoMatch[3]}`;
00053:   }
00054: 
00055:   const brMatch = raw.match(
00056:     /^(\d{2})\/(\d{2})\/(\d{4})/
00057:   );
00058: 
00059:   if (brMatch) {
00060:     return `${brMatch[3]}-${brMatch[2]}-${brMatch[1]}`;
00061:   }
00062: 
00063:   return "";
00064: }

00074:     draw?.closeDate

00087: function groupFromDezena(value) {
00088:   const digits = String(value ?? "")
00089:     .replace(/\D+/g, "")
00090:     .padStart(2, "0")
00091:     .slice(-2);
00092: 
00093:   if (!/^\d{2}$/.test(digits)) {
00094:     return null;
00095:   }
00096: 
00097:   const dezenaRaw = Number(digits);
00098: 
00099:   if (!Number.isFinite(dezenaRaw)) {
00100:     return null;
00101:   }
00102: 
00103:   const dezena = dezenaRaw === 0
00104:     ? 100
00105:     : dezenaRaw;
00106: 
00107:   const group = Math.ceil(dezena / 4);
00108: 
00109:   return group >= 1 && group <= GROUPS_K
00110:     ? group
00111:     : null;
00112: }

00114: function pickPrizePosition(prize) {

00116:     prize?.position,

00118:     prize?.pos,
00119:     prize?.colocacao,
00120:   ];
00121: 
00122:   for (const value of candidates) {
00123:     const position = Number(value);
00124: 
00125:     if (Number.isFinite(position)) {
00126:       return position;
00127:     }
00128:   }
00129: 
00130:   return null;
00131: }

00133: function pickPrizeGroup(prize) {
00134:   const directCandidates = [
00135:     prize?.grupo,
00136:     prize?.group,
00137:     prize?.grupo2,
00138:     prize?.group2,
00139:     prize?.animal_grupo,
00140:     prize?.grupo_animal,
00141:     prize?.grupoAnimal,
00142:     prize?.g,
00143:   ];
00144: 
00145:   for (const value of directCandidates) {
00146:     const group = Number(value);
00147: 
00148:     if (
00149:       Number.isFinite(group) &&
00150:       group >= 1 &&
00151:       group <= GROUPS_K
00152:     ) {
00153:       return group;
00154:     }
00155:   }
00156: 
00157:   const numberCandidates = [
00158:     prize?.milhar,
00159:     prize?.milhar4,
00160:     prize?.numero,
00161:     prize?.number,
00162:     prize?.value,
00163:     prize?.resultado,
00164:     prize?.result,
00165:     prize?.premio,
00166:   ];
00167:

00169:     const digits = String(value ?? "")
00170:       .replace(/\D+/g, "");
00171: 
00172:     if (!digits) {
00173:       continue;
00174:     }
00175: 
00176:     const group = groupFromDezena(
00177:       digits.slice(-2)
00178:     );
00179: 
00180:     if (group != null) {
00181:       return group;
00182:     }
00183:   }
00184: 
00185:   return null;
00186: }

00188: function pickFirstPrizeGroup(draw) {

00192:     prizes.find(
00193:       (prize) =>
00194:         pickPrizePosition(prize) === 1
00195:     ) || prizes[0] || null;
00196: 
00197:   if (firstPrize) {
00198:     const group = pickPrizeGroup(firstPrize);
00199: 
00200:     if (group != null) {
00201:       return group;
00202:     }

00204: 
00205:   const directCandidates = [
00206:     draw?.grupo1,
00207:     draw?.group1,
00208:     draw?.primeiro_grupo,
00209:     draw?.first_grupo,
00210:     draw?.prize1_grupo,
00211:     draw?.prize1Grupo,
00212:     draw?.p1_grupo2,
00213:     draw?.p1Grupo2,
00214:     draw?.grupo_1,
00215:     draw?.grupoPrimeiro,
00216:     draw?.g1,
00217:   ];
00218: 
00219:   for (const value of directCandidates) {
00220:     const group = Number(value);
00221: 
00222:     if (
00223:       Number.isFinite(group) &&
00224:       group >= 1 &&
00225:       group <= GROUPS_K
00226:     ) {
00227:       return group;
00228:     }
00229:   }
00230: 
00231:   return null;
00232: }

00234: function drawTimestamp(draw) {
00235:   const ymd = pickDrawYmd(draw);
00236:   const hour = pickDrawHour(draw);
00237: 
00238:   if (!ymd || !hour) {
00239:     return Number.NaN;
00240:   }
00241:

00243:     `${ymd}T${hour}:00Z`
00244:   );

00247: function sortDraws(draws) {
00248:   return safeArray(draws)
00249:     .filter(Boolean)
00250:     .map((draw, index) => ({
00251:       draw,
00252:       index,
00253:       timestamp: drawTimestamp(draw),
00254:     }))
00255:     .filter(
00256:       (item) =>
00257:         Number.isFinite(item.timestamp)
00258:     )
00259:     .sort(
00260:       (a, b) =>
00261:         a.timestamp - b.timestamp ||
00262:         a.index - b.index
00263:     )
00264:     .map((item) => item.draw);
00265: }

00267: function createGroupMap() {
00268:   const map = new Map();
00269: 
00270:   for (
00271:     let group = 1;
00272:     group <= GROUPS_K;
00273:     group += 1
00274:   ) {
00275:     map.set(group, 0);
00276:   }
00277: 
00278:   return map;
00279: }
00280: 
00281: function increment(map, group) {
00282:   const value = Number(group);
00283: 
00284:   if (
00285:     !Number.isFinite(value) ||
00286:     value < 1 ||
00287:     value > GROUPS_K
00288:   ) {
00289:     return;
00290:   }
00291: 
00292:   map.set(
00293:     value,
00294:     Number(map.get(value) || 0) + 1
00295:   );
00296: }
00297: 
00298: function probabilityMap(
00299:   frequency,
00300:   samples,
00301:   alpha = LAPLACE_ALPHA
00302: ) {
00303:   const probabilities = new Map();
00304: 
00305:   const safeSamples = Math.max(
00306:     0,
00307:     Number(samples || 0)
00308:   );
00309: 
00310:   const safeAlpha = Math.max(
00311:     0,
00312:     Number(alpha || 0)
00313:   );
00314: 
00315:   const denominator =
00316:     safeSamples +
00317:     (safeAlpha * GROUPS_K);
00318: 
00319:   for (
00320:     let group = 1;
00321:     group <= GROUPS_K;
00322:     group += 1
00323:   ) {
00324:     const count = Number(
00325:       frequency.get(group) || 0
00326:     );
00327: 
00328:     probabilities.set(
00329:       group,
00330:       denominator > 0
00331:         ? (count + safeAlpha) / denominator
00332:         : 1 / GROUPS_K
00333:     );
00334:   }
00335: 
00336:   return probabilities;
00337: }
00338: 
00339: function mapToObject(map) {
00340:   return Object.fromEntries(
00341:     Array.from(map.entries()).map(
00342:       ([group, value]) => [
00343:         String(group).padStart(2, "0"),
00344:         value,
00345:       ]
00346:     )
00347:   );
00348: }

00350: function collectTransitionEvidence(input = {}) {
00351:   const draws = sortDraws(
00352:     input.drawsRange
00353:   );
00354: 
00355:   const drawLast =
00356:     input.drawLast || draws[draws.length - 1];
00357: 
00358:   const previousGroup =
00359:     pickFirstPrizeGroup(drawLast);
00360: 
00361:   const previousHour =
00362:     pickDrawHour(drawLast);
00363: 
00364:   const targetHour =
00365:     normalizeHour(
00366:       input.targetHourOverride
00367:     );
00368: 
00369:   const globalFrequency =
00370:     createGroupMap();
00371: 
00372:   const sameHourFrequency =
00373:     createGroupMap();
00374: 
00375:   const transitionFrequency =
00376:     createGroupMap();
00377: 
00378:   let globalSamples = 0;
00379:   let sameHourSamples = 0;
00380:   let transitionSamples = 0;
00381: 
00382:   for (const draw of draws) {
00383:     const group =
00384:       pickFirstPrizeGroup(draw);
00385: 
00386:     const hour =
00387:       pickDrawHour(draw);
00388: 
00389:     if (group == null) {
00390:       continue;
00391:     }
00392: 
00393:     globalSamples += 1;
00394:     increment(
00395:       globalFrequency,
00396:       group
00397:     );
00398: 
00399:     if (
00400:       targetHour &&
00401:       hour === targetHour
00402:     ) {
00403:       sameHourSamples += 1;
00404:       increment(
00405:         sameHourFrequency,
00406:         group
00407:       );
00408:     }
00409:   }
00410: 
00411:   for (
00412:     let index = 0;
00413:     index < draws.length - 1;
00414:     index += 1
00415:   ) {
00416:     const previousDraw =
00417:       draws[index];
00418: 
00419:     const nextDraw =
00420:       draws[index + 1];
00421: 
00422:     const historicalPreviousGroup =
00423:       pickFirstPrizeGroup(
00424:         previousDraw
00425:       );
00426: 
00427:     const historicalPreviousHour =
00428:       pickDrawHour(
00429:         previousDraw
00430:       );
00431: 
00432:     const historicalNextGroup =
00433:       pickFirstPrizeGroup(
00434:         nextDraw
00435:       );
00436: 
00437:     const historicalNextHour =
00438:       pickDrawHour(
00439:         nextDraw
00440:       );
00441: 
00442:     if (
00443:       historicalPreviousGroup == null ||
00444:       historicalNextGroup == null ||
00445:       !historicalPreviousHour ||
00446:       !historicalNextHour
00447:     ) {
00448:       continue;
00449:     }
00450: 
00451:     if (
00452:       Number(historicalPreviousGroup) !==
00453:       Number(previousGroup)
00454:     ) {
00455:       continue;
00456:     }
00457: 
00458:     if (
00459:       historicalPreviousHour !==
00460:       previousHour
00461:     ) {
00462:       continue;
00463:     }
00464: 
00465:     if (
00466:       targetHour &&
00467:       historicalNextHour !==
00468:       targetHour
00469:     ) {
00470:       continue;
00471:     }
00472: 
00473:     transitionSamples += 1;
00474: 
00475:     increment(
00476:       transitionFrequency,
00477:       historicalNextGroup
00478:     );
00479:   }
00480: 
00481:   const globalProbability =
00482:     probabilityMap(
00483:       globalFrequency,
00484:       globalSamples
00485:     );
00486: 
00487:   const sameHourProbability =
00488:     probabilityMap(
00489:       sameHourFrequency,
00490:       sameHourSamples
00491:     );
00492: 
00493:   const transitionProbability =
00494:     probabilityMap(
00495:       transitionFrequency,
00496:       transitionSamples
00497:     );
00498: 
00499:   const source =
00500:     transitionSamples > 0
00501:       ? "TRANSITION"
00502:       : sameHourSamples > 0
00503:         ? "SAME_HOUR_FALLBACK"
00504:         : "GLOBAL_FALLBACK";
00505: 
00506:   const selectedProbability =
00507:     source === "TRANSITION"
00508:       ? transitionProbability
00509:       : source === "SAME_HOUR_FALLBACK"
00510:         ? sameHourProbability
00511:         : globalProbability;
00512: 
00513:   const selectedFrequency =
00514:     source === "TRANSITION"
00515:       ? transitionFrequency
00516:       : source === "SAME_HOUR_FALLBACK"
00517:         ? sameHourFrequency
00518:         : globalFrequency;
00519: 
00520:   const selectedSamples =
00521:     source === "TRANSITION"
00522:       ? transitionSamples
00523:       : source === "SAME_HOUR_FALLBACK"
00524:         ? sameHourSamples
00525:         : globalSamples;
00526: 
00527:   const ranking = Array.from(
00528:     {
00529:       length: GROUPS_K,
00530:     },
00531:     (_, index) => {
00532:       const group = index + 1;
00533: 
00534:       return {
00535:         grupo: group,
00536:         scoreProb: Number(
00537:           selectedProbability.get(group) || 0
00538:         ),
00539:         score:
00540:           Number(
00541:             selectedProbability.get(group) || 0
00542:           ) * 1000,
00543:         frequency: Number(
00544:           selectedFrequency.get(group) || 0
00545:         ),
00546:         transitionProbability: Number(
00547:           transitionProbability.get(group) || 0
00548:         ),
00549:         sameHourProbability: Number(
00550:           sameHourProbability.get(group) || 0
00551:         ),
00552:         globalProbability: Number(
00553:           globalProbability.get(group) || 0
00554:         ),
00555:       };
00556:     }
00557:   ).sort((a, b) => {
00558:     if (b.score !== a.score) {
00559:       return b.score - a.score;
00560:     }
00561: 
00562:     if (b.frequency !== a.frequency) {
00563:       return b.frequency - a.frequency;
00564:     }
00565: 
00566:     return a.grupo - b.grupo;
00567:   });
00568: 
00569:   return {
00570:     enabled: true,
00571:     experiment: "E07",
00572:     version: 1,
00573:     source,
00574:     previous: {
00575:       ymd: pickDrawYmd(drawLast),
00576:       hour: previousHour,
00577:       group: previousGroup,
00578:     },
00579:     target: {
00580:       ymd: normalizeYmd(
00581:         input.targetYmdOverride
00582:       ),
00583:       hour: targetHour,
00584:     },
00585:     samples: {
00586:       global: globalSamples,
00587:       sameHour: sameHourSamples,
00588:       transition: transitionSamples,
00589:       selected: selectedSamples,
00590:     },
00591:     frequencies: {
00592:       global:
00593:         mapToObject(globalFrequency),
00594:       sameHour:
00595:         mapToObject(sameHourFrequency),
00596:       transition:
00597:         mapToObject(transitionFrequency),
00598:     },
00599:     probabilities: {
00600:       global:
00601:         mapToObject(globalProbability),
00602:       sameHour:
00603:         mapToObject(sameHourProbability),
00604:       transition:
00605:         mapToObject(transitionProbability),
00606:     },
00607:     ranking,
00608:   };
00609: }
00610: 
00611: function buildExperimentalTop(
00612:   evidence,
00613:   topN = 3
00614: ) {
00615:   return safeArray(
00616:     evidence?.ranking
00617:   )
00618:     .slice(
00619:       0,
00620:       Math.max(
00621:         1,
00622:         Number(topN || 3)
00623:       )
00624:     )
00625:     .map(
00626:       (item, index) => ({
00627:         rank: index + 1,
00628:         title:
00629:           index === 0
00630:             ? "Mais provavel"
00631:             : index === 1
00632:               ? "2o mais provavel"
00633:               : "3o mais provavel",
00634:         grupo: item.grupo,
00635:         scoreProb:
00636:           item.scoreProb,
00637:         rawScoreProb:
00638:           item.scoreProb,
00639:         score:
00640:           item.score,
00641:         probability:
00642:           item.scoreProb,
00643:         probCond:
00644:           item.transitionProbability,
00645:         probBase:
00646:           item.sameHourProbability,
00647:         freq:
00648:           item.frequency,
00649:         freqCond:
00650:           item.frequency,
00651:         freqBase: 0,
00652:         lateBonus: 0,
00653:         reasons: [
00654:           "Motor experimental E07",
00655:           `Fonte: ${evidence.source}`,
00656:           `Transicoes: ${evidence.samples.transition}`,
00657:           `Mesmo horario: ${evidence.samples.sameHour}`,
00658:           `Historico global: ${evidence.samples.global}`,
00659:           `Grupo anterior: ${String(
00660:             evidence.previous.group
00661:           ).padStart(2, "0")}`,
00662:           `Hora anterior: ${evidence.previous.hour}`,
00663:           `Hora alvo: ${evidence.target.hour}`,
00664:         ],
00665:         meta: {
00666:           experiment: "E07",
00667:           source:
00668:             evidence.source,
00669:           samples:
00670:             evidence.samples,
00671:           previous:
00672:             evidence.previous,
00673:           target:
00674:             evidence.target,
00675:         },
00676:       })
00677:     );
00678: }
00679: 
00680: function computeStatisticalTop3V4Experimental(
00681:   input = {}
00682: ) {
00683:   const baseline =
00684:     computeStatisticalTop3V3(
00685:       input
00686:     );
00687: 
00688:   const evidence =
00689:     collectTransitionEvidence(
00690:       input
00691:     );
00692: 
00693:   const experimentalTop =
00694:     buildExperimentalTop(
00695:       evidence,
00696:       input.topN || 3
00697:     );
00698: 
00699:   return {
00700:     ...baseline,
00701:     top:
00702:       experimentalTop.length
00703:         ? experimentalTop
00704:         : safeArray(baseline?.top),
00705:     ranking:
00706:       experimentalTop.length
00707:         ? experimentalTop
00708:         : safeArray(
00709:             baseline?.ranking
00710:           ),
00711:     baselineTop:
00712:       safeArray(baseline?.top),
00713:     experimental: {
00714:       enabled: true,
00715:       version: 4,
00716:       experiment: "E07",
00717:       noProductionInfluence: true,
00718:       evidence,
00719:     },
00720:     meta: {
00721:       ...(baseline?.meta || {}),
00722:       scenario:
00723:         "V4_EXPERIMENTAL_E07",
00724:       experimental: {
00725:         experiment: "E07",
00726:         source:
00727:           evidence.source,
00728:         samples:
00729:           evidence.samples,
00730:       },
00731:     },
00732:   };
00733: }
00734: 
00735: module.exports = {
00736:   collectTransitionEvidence,
00737:   computeStatisticalTop3V4Experimental,
00738: };

00013: 
00014: async function fetchNewDrawsAfter(db, lottery, lastProcessedDrawId, limit = 25) {
00015:   if (!lastProcessedDrawId) {
00016:     return {
00017:       requiresBootstrap: true,
00018:       draws: [],
00019:     };
00020:   }
00021: 
00022:   const admin = require("firebase-admin");
00023:   const DOC_ID = admin.firestore.FieldPath.documentId();
00024: 
00025:   const snap = await db
00026:     .collection("draws")
00027:     .where("lottery_key", "==", String(lottery).toUpperCase())
00028:     .orderBy(DOC_ID, "asc")
00029:     .startAfter(String(lastProcessedDrawId))
00030:     .limit(limit)
00031:     .get();
00032: 
00033:   return {
00034:     requiresBootstrap: false,
00035:     draws: snap.docs.map((doc) => ({
00036:       id: doc.id,
00037:       ...doc.data(),
00038:     })),
00039:   };
00040: }
00041: 
00042: async function updateSnapshot({
00043:   lottery = "PT_RIO",
00044:   force = false,
00045: } = {}) {
00046: 
00047:   const db = getAdminDb();
00048: 
00049:   // Bounds atuais do Firestore
00050:   const { result: bounds } = await computeBounds(db, lottery);
00051: 
00052:   // Snapshot existente
00053:   const current = await readStatisticsSnapshot({
00054:     uf: lottery,
00055:     scope: "dashboard_full",
00056:   });
00057: 
00058:   // Metadata de controle incremental
00059:   const metadata = await readSnapshotMetadata(lottery);
00060: 
00061:   const lastProcessedDrawId = metadata?.lastProcessedDrawId || null;
00062:   const latestDrawId = bounds?.maxDocId || null;
00063: 
00064:   const upToDate =
00065:     !force &&
00066:     lastProcessedDrawId &&
00067:     latestDrawId &&
00068:     String(lastProcessedDrawId) === String(latestDrawId);
00069: 
00070:   if (upToDate) {
00071:     return {
00072:       ok: true,
00073:       lottery,
00074:       mode: "noop",
00075:       reason: "snapshot_already_up_to_date",
00076:       bounds,
00077:       metadata,
00078:       processed: 0,
00079:     };
00080:   }
00081: 
00082:   const pending = await fetchNewDrawsAfter(
00083:     db,
00084:     lottery,
00085:     lastProcessedDrawId,
00086:     25
00087:   );
00088: 
00089:   await writeSnapshotMetadata(lottery, {
00090:     lastKnownMinYmd: bounds?.minYmd || null,
00091:     lastKnownMaxYmd: bounds?.maxYmd || null,
00092:     latestDrawId,
00093:     lastCheckAt: new Date().toISOString(),
00094:     pendingIncrementalEngine: true,
00095:     pendingNewDrawsCount: pending.draws.length,
00096:     requiresBootstrap: pending.requiresBootstrap,
00097:   });
00098: 
00099:   return {
00100:     ok: true,
00101:     lottery,
00102:     mode: pending.requiresBootstrap
00103:       ? "bootstrap_required"
00104:       : force
00105:       ? "force_pending"
00106:       : "incremental_pending",
00107:     bounds,
00108:     snapshot: current.data || null,
00109:     metadata,
00110:     pendingNewDraws: pending.draws,
00111:     pendingNewDrawsCount: pending.draws.length,
00112:     requiresBootstrap: pending.requiresBootstrap,
00113:     processed: 0,
00114:     nextStep: pending.requiresBootstrap
00115:       ? "IMPLEMENT_BOOTSTRAP_SNAPSHOT"
00116:       : "IMPLEMENT_APPLY_NEW_DRAWS",
00117:   };
00118: }
00119: 
00120: module.exports = {
00121:   updateSnapshot,
00122: };

00033:

00238: function sortDraws(draws = []) {
00239:   return [...draws].sort((a, b) => {
00240:     const aKey =
00241:       `${a.ymd}T${a.closeHour}T${a.drawId}`;
00242: 
00243:     const bKey =
00244:       `${b.ymd}T${b.closeHour}T${b.drawId}`;
00245: 
00246:     return aKey.localeCompare(bKey);
00247:   });
00248: }

00250: function deduplicateDraws(draws = []) {
00251:   const map = new Map();

00253:   for (const raw of safeArray(draws)) {

00256:     if (!draw) {
00257:       continue;
00258:     }
00259: 
00260:     map.set(draw.drawId, draw);
00261:   }
00262: 
00263:   return sortDraws(
00264:     Array.from(map.values())
00265:   );
00266: }

00489: function encodeCompactPrize(prize = {}) {
00490:   const normalized = normalizePrize(prize);
00491: 
00492:   if (!normalized) {
00493:     return null;
00494:   }

00497:     i: normalized.id,
00498:     o: normalized.position,
00499:     g: normalized.grupo,
00500:     m: normalized.milhar,

00502:     d: normalized.dezena,
00503:   };

00506: function decodeCompactPrize(row) {
00507:   if (
00508:     !row ||
00509:     typeof row !== "object" ||
00510:     Array.isArray(row)
00511:   ) {
00512:     return null;
00513:   }
00514: 
00515:   return normalizePrize({
00516:     id: row.i,
00517:     position: row.o,
00518:     grupo: row.g,
00519:     milhar: row.m,
00520:     centena: row.c,
00521:     dezena: row.d,
00522:   });

00525: function encodeCompactDraw(draw = {}) {
00526:   const normalized = normalizeDraw(draw);
00527: 
00528:   if (!normalized) {
00529:     return null;
00530:   }
00531: 
00532:   return {
00533:     d: normalized.drawId,
00534:     y: normalized.ymd,
00535:     h: normalized.closeHour,
00536:     k: normalized.lotteryKey,
00537:     c: normalized.lotteryCode,
00538:     p: safeArray(normalized.prizes)
00539:       .map(encodeCompactPrize)
00540:       .filter(Boolean),
00541:   };
00542: }

00544: function decodeCompactDraw(row) {
00545:   if (

00549:   ) {

00558:     lotteryKey: row.k,
00559:     lotteryCode: row.c,
00560:     prizes: safeArray(row.p)
00561:       .map(decodeCompactPrize)
00562:       .filter(Boolean),

00615: async function readCompactHistoryYear(
00616:   lotteryKey,
00617:   year,
00618:   dependencies = {}
00619: ) {

00634:       year: normalizedYear,
00635:       draws: [],
00636:       data: null,

00638:   }
00639:

00641: 
00642:   return {
00643:     exists: true,
00644:     lotteryKey: key,
00645:     year: normalizedYear,
00646:     draws: deduplicateDraws(
00647:       safeArray(data.rows)
00648:         .map(decodeCompactDraw)
00649:         .filter(Boolean)
00650:     ),
00651:     data,
00652:   };
00653: }

00655: async function writeCompactHistoryYear(
00656:   lotteryKey,
00657:   year,
00658:   draws,
00659:   dependencies = {}
00660: ) {
00661:   const database = resolveDb(dependencies);
00662:   const key = normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey);
00663:   const normalizedYear = normalizeYear(year);
00664: 
00665:   const normalizedDraws = deduplicateDraws(
00666:     draws
00667:   ).filter(
00668:     (draw) =>
00669:       draw.ymd.slice(0, 4) === normalizedYear
00670:   );
00671: 
00672:   const first =
00673:     normalizedDraws[0] || null;
00674: 
00675:   const last =
00676:     normalizedDraws[
00677:       normalizedDraws.length - 1
00678:     ] || null;
00679: 
00680:   const payload = {
00681:     schemaVersion: COMPACT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
00682:     lotteryKey: key,
00683:     year: normalizedYear,
00684:     drawCount: normalizedDraws.length,
00685:     firstYmd: first?.ymd || null,
00686:     lastYmd: last?.ymd || null,
00687:     firstDrawId:
00688:       first?.drawId || null,
00689:     lastDrawId:
00690:       last?.drawId || null,
00691:     rows: normalizedDraws
00692:       .map(encodeCompactDraw)
00693:       .filter(Boolean),
00694:     updatedAt: new Date(),
00695:   };
00696: 
00697:   await compactYearRef(
00698:     database,
00699:     key,
00700:     normalizedYear
00701:   ).set(
00702:     payload,
00703:     {
00704:       merge: false,
00705:     }
00706:   );
00707: 
00708:   return payload;
00709: }
00710: 
00711: async function upsertCompactHistoryYear(
00712:   lotteryKey,
00713:   year,
00714:   newDraws,
00715:   dependencies = {}
00716: ) {
00717:   const current =
00718:     await readCompactHistoryYear(
00719:       lotteryKey,
00720:       year,
00721:       dependencies
00722:     );
00723: 
00724:   const merged = deduplicateDraws([
00725:     ...current.draws,
00726:     ...safeArray(newDraws),
00727:   ]);
00728: 
00729:   const payload =
00730:     await writeCompactHistoryYear(
00731:       lotteryKey,
00732:       year,
00733:       merged,
00734:       dependencies
00735:     );
00736: 
00737:   return {
00738:     ...payload,
00739:     previousDrawCount:
00740:       current.draws.length,
00741:   };
00742: }

00744: async function readCompactFullHistory(
00745:   lotteryKey,
00746:   dependencies = {}
00747: ) {
00748:   const key = normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey);
00749: 
00750:   const manifestResult =
00751:     await readCompactManifest(
00752:       key,
00753:       dependencies
00754:     );
00755: 
00756:   const manifest =
00757:     manifestResult?.data || null;
00758: 
00759:   if (
00760:     manifestResult?.exists !== true ||
00761:     manifest?.status !== "complete"
00762:   ) {
00763:     return [];
00764:   }
00765: 
00766:   const years = safeArray(
00767:     manifest.years
00768:   )
00769:     .map((year) =>
00770:       String(year || "").trim()
00771:     )
00772:     .filter(
00773:       (year) =>
00774:         /^\d{4}$/.test(year)
00775:     )
00776:     .sort();
00777: 
00778:   if (!years.length) {
00779:     return [];
00780:   }
00781: 
00782:   const yearlyResults =
00783:     await Promise.all(
00784:       years.map(
00785:         (year) =>
00786:           readCompactHistoryYear(
00787:             key,
00788:             year,
00789:             dependencies
00790:           )
00791:       )
00792:     );
00793: 
00794:   if (
00795:     yearlyResults.some(
00796:       (result) =>
00797:         result.exists !== true
00798:     )
00799:   ) {
00800:     throw new Error(
00801:       "Snapshot compacto possui ano ausente."
00802:     );
00803:   }
00804: 
00805:   const draws = deduplicateDraws(
00806:     yearlyResults.flatMap(
00807:       (result) =>
00808:         result.draws
00809:     )
00810:   );
00811: 
00812:   const expectedTotal = Number(
00813:     manifest.totalDraws || 0
00814:   );
00815: 
00816:   if (
00817:     expectedTotal > 0 &&
00818:     draws.length !== expectedTotal
00819:   ) {
00820:     throw new Error(
00821:       "Snapshot compacto inconsistente: " +
00822:       `esperado=${expectedTotal}; ` +
00823:       `carregado=${draws.length}.`
00824:     );
00825:   }
00826: 
00827:   return draws;
00828: }
00829: 
00830: async function readLegacyFullHistory(
00831:   lotteryKey,
00832:   dependencies = {}
00833: ) {
00834:   const months = await listHistoryMonths(
00835:     lotteryKey,
00836:     dependencies
00837:   );
00838: 
00839:   const draws = [];
00840: 
00841:   for (const month of months) {
00842:     draws.push(
00843:       ...safeArray(month.draws)
00844:     );
00845:   }
00846: 
00847:   return deduplicateDraws(draws);
00848: }
00849: 
00850: async function readFullHistory(
00851:   lotteryKey,
00852:   dependencies = {}
00853: ) {
00854:   if (
00855:     dependencies.forceLegacy !== true
00856:   ) {
00857:     try {
00858:       const compact =
00859:         await readCompactFullHistory(
00860:           lotteryKey,
00861:           dependencies
00862:         );
00863: 
00864:       if (compact.length) {
00865:         return compact;
00866:       }
00867:     } catch (error) {
00868:       console.warn(
00869:         "[TOP3-HISTORY] Snapshot compacto indisponível; " +
00870:         "usando histórico mensal:",
00871:         error?.message || error
00872:       );
00873:     }
00874:   }
00875: 
00876:   return readLegacyFullHistory(
00877:     lotteryKey,
00878:     dependencies
00879:   );
00880: }

00042:   database,
00043:   drawDoc

00070: async function loadImportedDraws(
00071:   importResult = {},
00072:   dependencies = {}
00073: ) {
00074:   const database = resolveDb(dependencies);
00075: 
00076:   const lotteryKey = normalizeLotteryKey(
00077:     importResult.lotteryKey
00078:   );
00079: 
00080:   const date = validYmd(
00081:     importResult.date
00082:   );
00083: 
00084:   const targetDrawIds = safeArray(
00085:     importResult.targetDrawIds
00086:   )
00087:     .map((value) => String(value || "").trim())
00088:     .filter(Boolean);
00089: 
00090:   const docs = [];
00091: 
00092:   if (targetDrawIds.length) {
00093:     const snapshots = await Promise.all(
00094:       targetDrawIds.map(
00095:         (drawId) =>

00097:             .collection("draws")
00098:             .doc(drawId)
00099:             .get()
00100:       )
00101:     );
00102: 
00103:     docs.push(
00104:       ...snapshots.filter(
00105:         (snap) => snap.exists
00106:       )
00107:     );
00108:   } else if (date) {
00109:     const snap = await database
00110:       .collection("draws")
00111:       .where("lottery_key", "==", lotteryKey)
00112:       .where("ymd", "==", date)
00113:       .get();
00114: 
00115:     docs.push(...snap.docs);
00116:   }
00117: 
00118:   const loadedDraws = await Promise.all(
00119:     docs.map(
00120:       (doc) =>
00121:         readDrawWithPrizes(
00122:           database,
00123:           doc
00124:         )
00125:     )
00126:   );
00127: 
00128:   const draws = loadedDraws.filter((draw) => {
00129:     if (!draw) {
00130:       return false;
00131:     }
00132: 
00133:     const drawLotteryKey = normalizeLotteryKey(
00134:       draw.lottery_key ||
00135:       draw.lotteryKey
00136:     );
00137: 
00138:     if (drawLotteryKey !== lotteryKey) {
00139:       return false;
00140:     }
00141: 
00142:     if (
00143:       date &&
00144:       String(draw.ymd || draw.date || "") !== date
00145:     ) {
00146:       return false;
00147:     }
00148: 
00149:     return true;
00150:   });
00151: 
00152:   return deduplicateDraws(draws);
00153: }

00155: function summarizeMonths(months = []) {
00156:   const ordered = [...safeArray(months)]
00157:     .sort((a, b) =>
00158:       String(a.yearMonth || a.id)
00159:         .localeCompare(
00160:           String(b.yearMonth || b.id)
00161:         )
00162:     );
00163: 
00164:   const allDraws = deduplicateDraws(
00165:     ordered.flatMap(
00166:       (month) =>
00167:         safeArray(month.draws)
00168:     )
00169:   );
00170: 
00171:   const first = allDraws[0] || null;
00172:   const last =
00173:     allDraws[allDraws.length - 1] || null;
00174: 
00175:   return {
00176:     totalDraws: allDraws.length,
00177:     monthCount: ordered.length,
00178:     months: ordered.map(
00179:       (month) =>
00180:         month.yearMonth || month.id
00181:     ),
00182:     firstYmd: first?.ymd || null,
00183:     lastYmd: last?.ymd || null,

00267:         ok: true,
00268:         skipped: true,
00269:         reason:
00270:           importResult.blocked === true
00271:             ? "import_blocked"
00272:             : "invalid_date",
00273:       };
00274:     }

00276:     const metadataResult = await loadMetadata(
00277:       lotteryKey,
00278:       dependencies.repositoryDependencies || {}
00279:     );

00282:       metadataResult?.data || null;
00283:

00319:         skipped: true,
00320:         reason: "no_draws_found",
00321:       };
00322:     }

00324:     const byMonth = new Map();
00325: 
00326:     for (const draw of draws) {
00327:       const yearMonth = draw.ymd.slice(0, 7);
00328:

00330:         byMonth.set(yearMonth, []);
00331:       }

00334:     }

00362:           payload?.firstDrawId || null,
00363:         lastDrawId:
00364:           payload?.lastDrawId || null,

00369:       metadata?.months

00372:         String(value || "").trim()
00373:       )
00374:       .filter(Boolean);

00377:       metadataMonths
00378:     );
00379: 
00380:     for (const item of updatedMonths) {
00381:       monthSet.add(item.yearMonth);
00382:     }

00385:       Array.from(monthSet).sort();

00388:       updatedMonths.reduce(
00389:         (total, item) =>
00390:           total +
00391:           Number(item.drawCount || 0) -
00392:           Number(
00393:             item.previousDrawCount || 0

00396:       );

00399:       0,
00400:       Number(metadata?.totalDraws || 0) +
00401:       totalDelta

00409:               metadata.firstDrawId || null,
00410:           }

00417:                 item.firstDrawId || null,
00418:             }
00419:           : null
00420:       ),
00421:     ]
00422:       .filter(Boolean)
00423:       .sort((a, b) =>
00424:         String(a.ymd)
00425:           .localeCompare(String(b.ymd))
00426:       );

00429:       metadata?.lastYmd

00431:             ymd: metadata.lastYmd,
00432:             drawId:
00433:               metadata.lastDrawId || null,
00434:           }

00437:         item.lastYmd

00439:               ymd: item.lastYmd,

00441:                 item.lastDrawId || null,

00444:       ),

00448:         String(a.ymd)
00449:           .localeCompare(String(b.ymd))
00450:       );
00451: 
00452:     const first =
00453:       firstCandidates[0] || null;
00454: 
00455:     const last =
00456:       lastCandidates[
00457:         lastCandidates.length - 1
00458:       ] || null;

00473:         last?.drawId || null,

00477:     let compactError = null;

00551:           dependencies.repositoryDependencies || {}

00553:

00555:       } catch (error) {

00647:     const markedStale = await markHistoryStale(

00650:       dependencies

00661:           error ||

00074: function normalizeLotteryKey(value) {

00076:     .trim()
00077:     .toUpperCase() || "PT_RIO";

00080: function normalizeYmd(value) {
00081:   const text = String(value || "").trim();
00082: 
00083:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(text)) {
00084:     throw new Error(
00085:       "Data inválida. Utilize o formato YYYY-MM-DD."
00086:     );
00087:   }
00088: 
00089:   return text;
00090: }

00092: function normalizeHour(value) {
00093:   const text = String(value || "")
00094:     .trim()
00095:     .replace(/[hH]/g, ":");
00096: 
00097:   const match = text.match(
00098:     /^(\d{1,2})(?::?(\d{2}))?$/
00099:   );
00100: 
00101:   if (!match) {
00102:     throw new Error(
00103:       "Horário inválido. Utilize HH:MM."
00104:     );
00105:   }
00106: 
00107:   const hour = Number(match[1]);
00108:   const minute = Number(match[2] || 0);
00109: 
00110:   if (
00111:     hour < 0 ||
00112:     hour > 23 ||
00113:     minute < 0 ||
00114:     minute > 59
00115:   ) {
00116:     throw new Error("Horário fora do intervalo válido.");
00117:   }
00118: 
00119:   return `${String(hour).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(
00120:     minute
00121:   ).padStart(2, "0")}`;
00122: }

00124: function dateHourKey(ymd, hour) {
00125:   return `${ymd}T${normalizeHour(hour)}`;

00128: function subtractDaysYmd(ymd, days) {
00129:   const safeDays = Math.max(
00130:     1,
00131:     Math.min(1460, Number(days || 180))
00132:   );
00133: 
00134:   const date = new Date(`${ymd}T12:00:00Z`);
00135: 
00136:   if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
00137:     throw new Error(
00138:       "Não foi possível calcular a janela histórica."
00139:     );
00140:   }
00141: 
00142:   date.setUTCDate(
00143:     date.getUTCDate() - safeDays
00144:   );
00145: 
00146:   return date.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
00147: }

00167: async function loadPredictionHistory({
00168:   lotteryKey,
00169:   date,
00170:   input = {},
00171:   dependencies = {},
00172: } = {}) {
00173:   const requestedSource = normalizeHistorySource(
00174:     input.historySource
00175:   );

00177:   const loadMetadata =
00178:     dependencies.readHistoryMetadata ||

00182:     dependencies.readFullHistory ||
00183:     readFullHistory;

00186:     dependencies.fetchDraws ||
00187:     fetchDrawsWithPrizesByRange;
