00190:     requestedSource === "auto" ||
00191:     requestedSource === "snapshot"
00192:   ) {
00193:     const metadataResult = await loadMetadata(
00194:       lotteryKey,
00195:       dependencies.historyDependencies || {}
00196:     );
00197: 
00198:     const metadata =
00199:       metadataResult?.data || null;
00200: 
00201:     const snapshotReady =
00202:       metadataResult?.exists === true &&
00203:       metadata?.bootstrapStatus === "complete";
00204: 
00205:     if (snapshotReady) {
00206:       const draws = await loadFullHistory(
00207:         lotteryKey,
00208:         dependencies.historyDependencies || {}
00209:       );
00210:

00212:         const expectedTotal = Number(
00213:           metadata?.totalDraws || 0

00215: 
00216:         if (
00217:           expectedTotal > 0 &&
00218:           draws.length !== expectedTotal
00219:         ) {
00220:           throw new Error(
00221:             "Histórico TOP3 inconsistente: metadata.totalDraws=" +
00222:             `${expectedTotal}, carregados=${draws.length}.`
00223:           );
00224:         }
00225: 
00226:         return {
00227:           source: "snapshot",
00228:           draws,
00229:           metadata,
00230:           lookbackDays: null,
00231:           maxDraws: null,
00232:           startYmd:
00233:             metadata?.firstYmd || null,
00234:         };
00235:       }
00236:

00238:         "Metadata do histórico TOP3 está completo, mas nenhum draw foi carregado."
00239:       );
00240:     }

00244:         "Histórico TOP3 completo ainda não está disponível."
00245:       );

00250:     30,
00251:     Math.min(
00252:       1460,
00253:       Number(input.lookbackDays || 180)
00254:     )
00255:   );
00256: 
00257:   const maxDraws = Math.max(
00258:     100,
00259:     Math.min(
00260:       5000,
00261:       Number(input.maxDraws || 1200)
00262:     )
00263:   );
00264: 
00265:   const startYmd = subtractDaysYmd(
00266:     date,
00267:     lookbackDays
00268:   );
00269:

00271:     lottery: lotteryKey,
00272:     startYmd,
00273:     endYmd: date,
00274:     pageSize: Number(input.pageSize || 250),
00275:     maxDraws,
00276:     prizeConcurrency: Number(
00277:       input.prizeConcurrency || 24
00278:     ),
00279:   });
00280: 
00281:   return {
00282:     source: "range_fallback",
00283:     draws: extractDraws(rawDraws),
00284:     metadata: null,
00285:     lookbackDays,
00286:     maxDraws,
00287:     startYmd,
00288:   };

00332: function resolveNextTop3Slot({
00333:   lotteryKey,
00334:   ymd,
00335:   hour,
00336:   publicApi,
00337: }) {
00338:   if (
00339:     !publicApi ||
00340:     typeof publicApi.getNextSlotForLottery !== "function"
00341:   ) {
00342:     throw new Error(
00343:       "API pública TOP3 sem getNextSlotForLottery."
00344:     );
00345:   }
00346: 
00347:   const resolved =
00348:     publicApi.getNextSlotForLottery({
00349:       lotteryKey: normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey),
00350:       ymd: normalizeYmd(ymd),
00351:       hourBucket: normalizeHour(hour),
00352:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00353:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
00354:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
00355:     });
00356: 
00357:   const rawHour = String(
00358:     resolved?.hour ||
00359:     resolved?.hourBucket ||
00360:     ""
00361:   ).trim();

00363:   const hourMatch =
00364:     rawHour.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?::00|h)?$/i);
00365: 
00366:   const canonicalHour = hourMatch
00367:     ? `${String(Number(hourMatch[1])).padStart(2, "0")}:00`
00368:     : "";
00369: 
00370:   return {
00371:     ...resolved,
00372:     ymd: normalizeYmd(resolved?.ymd),
00373:     hour: canonicalHour,
00374:     hourBucket: canonicalHour,
00375:   };
00376: }
00377: 
00378: function scheduleForPublicProjection(
00379:   lotteryKey,
00380:   date
00381: ) {
00382:   if (normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey) === "FEDERAL") {
00383:     return [...FEDERAL_SCHEDULE];
00384:   }
00385: 
00386:   const parsed = new Date(`${date}T12:00:00Z`);
00387:   const dow = parsed.getUTCDay();
00388: 
00389:   if (dow === 3 || dow === 6) {
00390:     return [...PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT];
00391:   }
00392: 
00393:   return [...PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL];
00394: }
00395: 
00396: function buildPublicMilharesCols(
00397:   engineOutput,
00398:   expectedCols = 4,
00399:   perCol = 5
00400: ) {
00401:   const dezenas = Array.isArray(engineOutput?.dezenas)
00402:     ? engineOutput.dezenas
00403:     : [];
00404: 
00405:   const slots = Array.isArray(engineOutput?.slots)
00406:     ? engineOutput.slots
00407:     : [];
00408: 
00409:   const cols = [];
00410: 
00411:   for (const dezena of dezenas.slice(0, expectedCols)) {
00412:     const items = slots
00413:       .filter(
00414:         (slot) =>
00415:           String(slot?.dezena || "") ===
00416:           String(dezena || "")
00417:       )
00418:       .map(
00419:         (slot) =>
00420:           normalizePublicMilhar(slot?.milhar)
00421:       )
00422:       .filter(
00423:         (milhar) => /^\d{4}$/.test(milhar)
00424:       )
00425:       .slice(0, perCol);
00426: 
00427:     while (items.length < perCol) {
00428:       items.push("");
00429:     }
00430: 
00431:     cols.push({
00432:       dezena: String(dezena || ""),
00433:       items,
00434:     });
00435:   }
00436: 
00437:   while (cols.length < expectedCols) {
00438:     cols.push({
00439:       dezena: "",
00440:       items: Array(perCol).fill(""),
00441:     });
00442:   }
00443: 
00444:   return cols.slice(0, expectedCols);
00445: }
00446: 
00447: function buildTop3PublicSnapshot({
00448:   computedTop,
00449:   history,
00450:   lotteryKey,
00451:   date,
00452:   closeHour,
00453:   publicApi,
00454: }) {
00455:   const schedule = scheduleForPublicProjection(
00456:     lotteryKey,
00457:     date
00458:   );
00459: 
00460:   return (Array.isArray(computedTop)
00461:     ? computedTop
00462:     : []
00463:   )
00464:     .slice(0, 3)
00465:     .map((item, index) => {
00466:       const grupo = Number(item?.grupo);
00467: 
00468:       if (
00469:         !Number.isFinite(grupo) ||
00470:         grupo < 1 ||
00471:         grupo > 25
00472:       ) {
00473:         return null;
00474:       }
00475: 
00476:       const probability = Number(
00477:         item?.scoreProb ??
00478:         item?.probability ??
00479:         item?.confidence ??
00480:         0
00481:       );
00482: 
00483:       const prob =
00484:         probability > 1
00485:           ? probability / 100
00486:           : probability;
00487: 
00488:       const engineOutput =
00489:         publicApi.build20MilharesForGrupo({
00490:           rangeDraws: history,
00491:           analysisHourBucket: closeHour,
00492:           schedule,
00493:           grupo2: grupo,
00494:           targetYmd: date,
00495:         });
00496: 
00497:       const milharesCols =
00498:         buildPublicMilharesCols(
00499:           engineOutput,
00500:           4,
00501:           5
00502:         );
00503: 
00504:       const milhares20 = milharesCols
00505:         .flatMap((column) => column.items)
00506:         .filter(
00507:           (milhar) => /^\d{4}$/.test(milhar)
00508:         )
00509:         .slice(0, 20);
00510: 
00511:       return {
00512:         rank: index + 1,
00513:         grupo,
00514:         animal:
00515:           item?.animal ||
00516:           ANIMALS[
00517:             String(grupo).padStart(2, "0")
00518:           ] ||
00519:           "",
00520:         prob:
00521:           Number.isFinite(prob)
00522:             ? prob
00523:             : 0,
00524:         probPct:
00525:           Number.isFinite(prob)
00526:             ? Number((prob * 100).toFixed(4))
00527:             : 0,
00528:         milhares20,
00529:         milharesCols,
00530:         meta: item?.meta || null,
00531:       };
00532:     })
00533:     .filter(Boolean);
00534: }
00535: 
00536: async function saveTop3PublicProjection({
00537:   lotteryKey,
00538:   date,
00539:   closeHour,
00540:   snapshot,
00541:   engineVersion = "V3_STATISTICAL",
00542:   source = "backend-top3",
00543: }) {
00544:   const lottery = normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey);
00545:   const hour = normalizeHour(closeHour);
00546:   const hourCode = publicHourCode(hour);
00547: 
00548:   const id =
00549:     `${lottery}__${date}__${hourCode}`;
00550: 
00551:   const normalizedSnapshot =
00552:     Array.isArray(snapshot)
00553:       ? snapshot.slice(0, 3)
00554:       : [];
00555: 
00556:   if (!normalizedSnapshot.length) {
00557:     throw new Error(
00558:       "Snapshot público TOP3 vazio."
00559:     );
00560:   }
00561: 
00562:   const picks = normalizedSnapshot
00563:     .map((item) => Number(item?.grupo))
00564:     .filter(
00565:       (grupo) =>
00566:         Number.isFinite(grupo) &&
00567:         grupo >= 1 &&
00568:         grupo <= 25
00569:     )
00570:     .slice(0, 3);
00571: 
00572:   if (!picks.length) {
00573:     throw new Error(
00574:       "Picks públicos TOP3 inválidos."
00575:     );
00576:   }
00577: 
00578:   const database = getDb();
00579:   const ref = database
00580:     .collection("top3_predictions")
00581:     .doc(id);
00582: 
00583:   const now = Date.now();
00584: 
00585:   const payload = {
00586:     id,
00587:     lotteryKey: lottery,
00588:     targetYmd: date,
00589:     targetHour:
00590:       `${hourCode}h`,
00591:     targetKey:
00592:       `${date}_${hourCode}h`,
00593:     picks,
00594:     snapshot: normalizedSnapshot,
00595:     engineVersion,
00596:     status: "predicted",
00597:     resultGrupo: null,
00598:     resultMilhar: "",
00599:     resultAnimal: "",
00600:     hitType: "",
00601:     hitScore: 0,
00602:     hitPosition: -1,
00603:     matchedValue: "",
00604:     createdAt: now,
00605:     updatedAt: now,
00606:     createdBy: source,
00607:     source,
00608:   };
00609: 
00610:   return database.runTransaction(
00611:     async (transaction) => {
00612:       const current =
00613:         await transaction.get(ref);
00614: 
00615:       if (!current.exists) {
00616:         transaction.set(ref, payload);
00617: 
00618:         return {
00619:           ok: true,
00620:           created: true,
00621:           updated: false,
00622:           existing: false,
00623:           protected: false,
00624:           id,
00625:         };
00626:       }
00627: 
00628:       const currentData =
00629:         current.data() || {};
00630: 
00631:       const currentStatus = String(
00632:         currentData.status || ""
00633:       )
00634:         .trim()
00635:         .toLowerCase();
00636: 
00637:       if (currentStatus === "validated") {
00638:         return {
00639:           ok: true,
00640:           created: false,
00641:           updated: false,
00642:           existing: true,
00643:           protected: true,
00644:           reason: "ALREADY_VALIDATED",
00645:           id,
00646:         };
00647:       }
00648: 
00649:       const updatedPayload = {
00650:         ...payload,
00651:         createdAt:
00652:           currentData.createdAt ||
00653:           payload.createdAt,
00654:         updatedAt: Date.now(),
00655:         resultGrupo:
00656:           currentData.resultGrupo ?? null,
00657:         resultMilhar:
00658:           currentData.resultMilhar || "",
00659:         resultAnimal:
00660:           currentData.resultAnimal || "",
00661:         hitType:
00662:           currentData.hitType || "",
00663:         hitScore:
00664:           Number(currentData.hitScore || 0),
00665:         hitPosition:
00666:           Number.isFinite(
00667:             Number(currentData.hitPosition)
00668:           )
00669:             ? Number(currentData.hitPosition)
00670:             : -1,
00671:         matchedValue:
00672:           currentData.matchedValue || "",
00673:       };
00674: 
00675:       transaction.set(
00676:         ref,
00677:         updatedPayload,
00678:         { merge: true }
00679:       );
00680: 
00681:       return {
00682:         ok: true,
00683:         created: false,
00684:         updated: true,
00685:         existing: true,
00686:         protected: false,
00687:         id,
00688:       };
00689:     }
00690:   );
00691: }
00692: 
00693: function mapTop3ToPredictions(top = []) {
00694:   return (Array.isArray(top) ? top : [])
00695:     .slice(0, 3)
00696:     .map((item, index) => {
00697:       const grupoNumber = Number(item?.grupo);
00698:       const grupo = String(grupoNumber).padStart(2, "0");
00699: 
00700:       const probability = Number(
00701:         item?.scoreProb ??
00702:         item?.probability ??
00703:         item?.confidence ??
00704:         0
00705:       );
00706: 
00707:       const percentage =
00708:         probability > 0 && probability <= 1
00709:           ? probability * 100
00710:           : probability;
00711: 
00712:       return {
00713:         type: "grupo",
00714:         grupo,
00715:         animal:
00716:           item?.animal ||
00717:           ANIMALS[grupo] ||
00718:           null,
00719:         score: Number(
00720:           Number.isFinite(percentage)
00721:             ? percentage.toFixed(4)
00722:             : 0
00723:         ),
00724:         confidence: Number(
00725:           Number.isFinite(percentage)
00726:             ? percentage.toFixed(4)
00727:             : 0
00728:         ),
00729:         reasons: Array.isArray(item?.reasons)
00730:           ? item.reasons.filter(Boolean)
00731:           : [],
00732:         signals: {
00733:           engine: "top3_statistical_v3",
00734:           rankPosition: index + 1,
00735:           scoreProb: Number(item?.scoreProb || 0),
00736:           rawScore: Number(item?.score || 0),
00737:           meta: item?.meta || null,
00738:         },
00739:       };
00740:     })
00741:     .filter((item) => /^\d{2}$/.test(item.grupo));
00742: }
00743: 
00744: async function createTop3PredictionRun(
00745:   input = {},
00746:   dependencies = {}
00747: ) {
00748:   const lotteryKey = normalizeLotteryKey(
00749:     input.lotteryKey
00750:   );
00751: 
00752:   const date = normalizeYmd(input.date);
00753:   const closeHour = normalizeHour(input.closeHour);
00754: 
00755:   const computeTop3 =
00756:     dependencies.computeTop3 ||
00757:     computeStatisticalTop3V3;
00758: 
00759:   const persistRun =
00760:     dependencies.persistRun ||
00761:     createPredictionRun;
00762: 
00763:   const publicApi =
00764:     dependencies.publicApi ||
00765:     loadTop3PublicApi();
00766: 
00767:   const historyLoad = await loadPredictionHistory({
00768:     lotteryKey,
00769:     date,
00770:     input,
00771:     dependencies,
00772:   });
00773: 
00774:   const allDraws = extractDraws(
00775:     historyLoad.draws
00776:   );
00777: 
00778:   const {
00779:     source: historySource,
00780:     metadata: historyMetadata,
00781:     lookbackDays,
00782:     maxDraws,
00783:     startYmd,
00784:   } = historyLoad;
00785: 
00786:   if (!allDraws.length) {
00787:     throw new Error(
00788:       `Nenhum resultado encontrado para ${lotteryKey}.`
00789:     );
00790:   }
00791: 
00792:   const targetKey = dateHourKey(date, closeHour);
00793: 
00794:   const history = allDraws
00795:     .map((draw) => {
00796:       const ymd =
00797:         publicApi.pickDrawYMD(draw);
00798: 
00799:       const rawHour =
00800:         publicApi.pickDrawHour(draw);
00801: 
00802:       if (!ymd || !rawHour) {
00803:         return null;
00804:       }
00805: 
00806:       const hour =
00807:         normalizeHour(rawHour);
00808: 
00809:       return {
00810:         draw,
00811:         ymd,
00812:         hour,
00813:         key: `${ymd}T${hour}`,
00814:       };
00815:     })
00816:     .filter(
00817:       (item) =>
00818:         item &&
00819:         item.key < targetKey
00820:     )
00821:     .sort((a, b) =>
00822:       a.key.localeCompare(b.key)
00823:     )
00824:     .map((item) => item.draw);
00825: 
00826:   if (!history.length) {
00827:     throw new Error(
00828:       "Não existe histórico anterior ao horário solicitado."
00829:     );
00830:   }
00831: 
00832:   const drawLast = history[history.length - 1];
00833: 
00834:   const drawsToday = history.filter(
00835:     (draw) => publicApi.pickDrawYMD(draw) === date
00836:   );
00837: 
00838:   const computed = computeTop3({
00839:     lotteryKey,
00840:     drawsRange: history,
00841:     drawLast,
00842:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00843:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
00844:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
00845:     topN: 3,
00846:     targetYmdOverride: date,
00847:     targetHourOverride: closeHour,
00848:     drawsAlreadySorted: true,
00849:   });
00850: 
00851:   const predictions = mapTop3ToPredictions(
00852:     computed?.top
00853:   );
00854: 
00855:   const publicSnapshot =
00856:     buildTop3PublicSnapshot({
00857:       computedTop: computed?.top,
00858:       history,
00859:       lotteryKey,
00860:       date,
00861:       closeHour,
00862:       publicApi,
00863:     });
00864: 
00865:   if (!predictions.length) {
00866:     throw new Error(
00867:       "O motor TOP3 não produziu previsões válidas."
00868:     );
00869:   }
00870: 
00871:   if (
00872:     publicSnapshot.length !== 3 ||
00873:     publicSnapshot.some(
00874:       (item) =>
00875:         !Array.isArray(item?.milhares20) ||
00876:         item.milhares20.length !== 20
00877:     )
00878:   ) {
00879:     throw new Error(
00880:       "O motor TOP3 não produziu 20 milhares válidas para cada grupo."
00881:     );
00882:   }
00883: 
00884:   const metadata = {
00885:     ...(input.metadata || {}),
00886:     engine: "top3_statistical_v3",
00887:     historyDraws: history.length,
00888:     drawsToday: drawsToday.length,
00889:     historySource,
00890:     historyBootstrapStatus:
00891:       historyMetadata?.bootstrapStatus || null,
00892:     historyTotalStored:
00893:       Number(historyMetadata?.totalDraws || 0) || null,
00894:     lookbackDays,
00895:     maxDraws,
00896:     startYmd,
00897:     lastDrawYmd:
00898:       publicApi.pickDrawYMD(drawLast) || null,
00899:     lastDrawHour:
00900:       publicApi.pickDrawHour(drawLast) || null,
00901:     targetYmd: date,
00902:     targetHour: closeHour,
00903:     engineMeta: computed?.meta || null,
00904:   };
00905: 
00906:   const engine = {
00907:     name: "top3_statistical_v3",
00908:     historyDraws: history.length,
00909:     drawsToday: drawsToday.length,
00910:     historySource,
00911:     historyBootstrapStatus:
00912:       historyMetadata?.bootstrapStatus || null,
00913:     historyTotalStored:
00914:       Number(historyMetadata?.totalDraws || 0) || null,
00915:     lookbackDays,
00916:     maxDraws,
00917:     startYmd,
00918:     targetYmd: date,
00919:     targetHour: closeHour,
00920:     meta: computed?.meta || null,
00921:   };
00922: 
00923:   if (input.dryRun === true) {
00924:     return {
00925:       run: null,
00926:       predictions,
00927:       publicSnapshot,
00928:       engine,
00929:       dryRun: true,
00930:     };
00931:   }
00932: 
00933:   const source =
00934:     input.source || "backend-top3";
00935: 
00936:   const result = await persistRun({
00937:     lotteryKey,
00938:     date,
00939:     closeHour,
00940:     source,
00941:     algorithm: "top3_statistical_v3",
00942:     metadata,
00943:     predictions,
00944:   });
00945: 
00946:   const publicProjection =
00947:     await saveTop3PublicProjection({
00948:       lotteryKey,
00949:       date,
00950:       closeHour,
00951:       snapshot: publicSnapshot,
00952:       engineVersion: "V3_STATISTICAL",
00953:       source,
00954:     });
00955: 
00956:   return {
00957:     ...result,
00958:     publicSnapshot,
00959:     publicProjection,
00960:     engine,
00961:     dryRun: false,
00962:   };
00963: }
00964: 
00965: module.exports = {
00966:   createTop3PredictionRun,
00967:   mapTop3ToPredictions,
00968:   normalizeHour,
00969:   normalizeHistorySource,
00970:   loadPredictionHistory,
00971:   buildPublicMilharesCols,
00972:   buildTop3PublicSnapshot,
00973:   saveTop3PublicProjection,
00974:   resolveNextTop3Slot,
00975: };

00008: /* ======================================================

00011: 
00012: function normalizeLotteryKey(v) {
00013:   const s = String(v ?? "").trim().toUpperCase();
00014: 
00015:   if (["RJ", "RIO", "PT-RIO", "PT_RIO"].includes(s)) return "PT_RIO";
00016: 
00017:   if (["FED", "FEDERAL", "BR"].includes(s)) return "FEDERAL";
00018: 
00019:   if (["LOOK", "GO"].includes(s)) return "LOOK";
00020: 
00021:   if (["NACIONAL", "LT_NACIONAL", "LT-NACIONAL"].includes(s)) {
00022:     return "NACIONAL";
00023:   }
00024: 
00025:   return null;
00026: }
00027: 
00028: /* ======================================================
00029:    UTILS
00030: ====================================================== */
00031: 
00032: function pad2(n) {
00033:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
00034: }
00035: 
00036: function isISODateStrict(s) {
00037:   const str = String(s || "").trim();
00038:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(str)) return false;
00039: 
00040:   const [y, m, d] = str.split("-").map(Number);
00041:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
00042: 
00043:   return (
00044:     dt.getUTCFullYear() === y &&
00045:     dt.getUTCMonth() === m - 1 &&
00046:     dt.getUTCDate() === d
00047:   );
00048: }
00049: 
00050: /* ======================================================
00051:    DATE NORMALIZATION
00052: ====================================================== */
00053: 
00054: function normalizeToYMD(input) {
00055:   if (!input) return null;
00056: 
00057:   if (typeof input === "object" && typeof input.toDate === "function") {
00058:     const d = input.toDate();
00059:     if (!Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) {
00060:       return `${d.getUTCFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getUTCMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(
00061:         d.getUTCDate()
00062:       )}`;
00063:     }
00064:   }
00065: 
00066:   if (
00067:     typeof input === "object" &&
00068:     (Number.isFinite(Number(input.seconds)) ||
00069:       Number.isFinite(Number(input._seconds)))
00070:   ) {
00071:     const sec = Number.isFinite(Number(input.seconds))
00072:       ? Number(input.seconds)
00073:       : Number(input._seconds);
00074: 
00075:     const d = new Date(sec * 1000);
00076: 
00077:     if (!Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) {
00078:       return `${d.getUTCFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getUTCMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(
00079:         d.getUTCDate()
00080:       )}`;
00081:     }
00082:   }
00083: 
00084:   if (input instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(input.getTime())) {
00085:     return `${input.getUTCFullYear()}-${pad2(input.getUTCMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(
00086:       input.getUTCDate()
00087:     )}`;
00088:   }
00089: 
00090:   const s = String(input || "").trim();
00091:   if (!s) return null;
00092: 
00093:   const iso = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
00094:   if (iso) return `${iso[1]}-${iso[2]}-${iso[3]}`;
00095: 
00096:   const br = s.match(/^(\d{2})\/(\d{2})\/(\d{4})$/);
00097:   if (br) return `${br[3]}-${br[2]}-${br[1]}`;
00098: 
00099:   return null;
00100: }
00101: 
00102: /* ======================================================
00103:    DOC SCAN
00104: ====================================================== */
00105: 
00106: function pickMinMaxFromDocs(docs) {
00107:   let minYmd = null;
00108:   let maxYmd = null;
00109: 
00110:   for (const doc of docs || []) {
00111:     const d = doc?.data ? doc.data() : doc || {};
00112: 
00113:     const y =
00114:       d?.ymd ||
00115:       normalizeToYMD(
00116:         d?.date ?? d?.data ?? d?.dt ?? d?.draw_date ?? d?.close_date
00117:       );
00118: 
00119:     if (!y || !isISODateStrict(y)) continue;
00120: 
00121:     if (!minYmd || y < minYmd) minYmd = y;
00122:     if (!maxYmd || y > maxYmd) maxYmd = y;
00123:   }
00124: 
00125:   return { minYmd, maxYmd };
00126: }
00127: 
00128: /* ======================================================
00129:    FIRESTORE INDEX ERROR DETECTOR
00130: ====================================================== */
00131: 
00132: function isIndexError(err) {
00133:   const msg = String(err?.message || "").toLowerCase();
00134:   const code = String(err?.code || "").toLowerCase();
00135: 
00136:   return (
00137:     code.includes("failed-precondition") ||
00138:     msg.includes("failed_precondition") ||
00139:     (msg.includes("index") && msg.includes("create")) ||
00140:     (msg.includes("requires") && msg.includes("index"))
00141:   );
00142: }
00143: 
00144: /* ======================================================
00145:    BOUNDS BY FIELD
00146: ====================================================== */
00147: 
00148: async function tryBoundsByField(db, lotteryKey, field, scanLimit = 50) {
00149:   const DOC_ID = admin.firestore.FieldPath.documentId();
00150: 
00151:   const base = db.collection("draws").where("lottery_key", "==", lotteryKey);
00152: 
00153:   const ascSnap = await base
00154:     .orderBy(field, "asc")
00155:     .orderBy(DOC_ID, "asc")
00156:     .limit(scanLimit)
00157:     .get();
00158: 
00159:   const descSnap = await base
00160:     .orderBy(field, "desc")
00161:     .orderBy(DOC_ID, "desc")
00162:     .limit(scanLimit)
00163:     .get();
00164: 
00165:   if (ascSnap.empty || descSnap.empty) {
00166:     return {
00167:       ok: false,
00168:       source: `where(lottery_key)+orderBy(${field})+docId(scan=${scanLimit})`,
00169:     };
00170:   }
00171: 
00172:   function pickFirstValidYmdFromDocs(docs, fieldName) {
00173:     for (const doc of docs || []) {
00174:       const v = doc?.data()?.[fieldName];
00175:       const y = normalizeToYMD(v);
00176: 
00177:       if (y && isISODateStrict(y)) {
00178:         return { ymd: y, docId: doc.id, raw: v };
00179:       }
00180:     }
00181: 
00182:     return { ymd: null, docId: null, raw: null };
00183:   }
00184: 
00185:   const minPick = pickFirstValidYmdFromDocs(ascSnap.docs, field);
00186:   const maxPick = pickFirstValidYmdFromDocs(descSnap.docs, field);
00187: 
00188:   return {
00189:     ok: !!(minPick.ymd && maxPick.ymd),
00190:     minYmd: minPick.ymd,
00191:     maxYmd: maxPick.ymd,
00192:     source: `where(lottery_key)+orderBy(${field})+docId(scan=${scanLimit})`,
00193:     minDocId: minPick.docId,
00194:     maxDocId: maxPick.docId,
00195:   };
00196: }
00197: 
00198: /* ======================================================
00199:    FALLBACK
00200: ====================================================== */
00201: 
00202: async function fallbackBoundsByDocIdEdges(db, lotteryKey, edgeLimit) {
00203:   const DOC_ID = admin.firestore.FieldPath.documentId();
00204: 
00205:   const base = db.collection("draws").where("lottery_key", "==", lotteryKey);
00206: 
00207:   const ascSnap = await base.orderBy(DOC_ID, "asc").limit(edgeLimit).get();
00208:   const descSnap = await base.orderBy(DOC_ID, "asc").limitToLast(edgeLimit).get();
00209: 
00210:   const merged = [...ascSnap.docs, ...descSnap.docs];
00211: 
00212:   const { minYmd, maxYmd } = pickMinMaxFromDocs(merged);
00213: 
00214:   return {
00215:     ok: !!(minYmd && maxYmd),
00216:     minYmd: minYmd || null,
00217:     maxYmd: maxYmd || null,
00218:     source: `fallback_edges_docId(limit=${edgeLimit})`,
00219:     sampleCount: merged.length,
00220:     firstDocId: ascSnap.docs[0]?.id || null,
00221:     lastDocId: descSnap.docs[descSnap.docs.length - 1]?.id || null,
00222:   };
00223: }
00224: 
00225: /* ======================================================
00226:    COMPUTE BOUNDS
00227: ====================================================== */
00228: 
00229: async function computeBounds(db, lotteryKey, opts = {}) {
00230:   const lk = normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey);
00231: 
00232:   const scanLimit = Number.isFinite(Number(opts.scanLimit))
00233:     ? Math.max(10, Number(opts.scanLimit))
00234:     : 50;
00235: 
00236:   const edgeLimit = Number.isFinite(Number(opts.edgeLimit))
00237:     ? Math.max(200, Number(opts.edgeLimit))
00238:     : 800;
00239: 
00240:   let result = null;
00241:   let warn = null;
00242: 
00243:   try {
00244:     result = await tryBoundsByField(db, lk, "ymd", scanLimit);
00245:   } catch (e) {
00246:     warn = e;
00247:   }
00248: 
00249:   if (!result || !result.ok) {
00250:     try {
00251:       const r2 = await tryBoundsByField(db, lk, "date", scanLimit);
00252:       if (r2?.minYmd && r2?.maxYmd) {
00253:         result = { ...r2, source: r2.source + " (normalized)" };
00254:       }
00255:     } catch (e) {
00256:       warn = warn || e;
00257:     }
00258:   }
00259: 
00260:   if (!result || !result.ok) {
00261:     result = await fallbackBoundsByDocIdEdges(db, lk, edgeLimit);
00262:   }
00263: 
00264:   return { lk, result, warn };
00265: }
00266: 
00267: /* ======================================================
00268:    API
00269: ====================================================== */
00270: 
00271: router.get("/bounds", async (req, res) => {
00272:   const lottery = normalizeLotteryKey(req.query.lottery || req.query.uf);
00273: 
00274:   if (!lottery) {
00275:     return res.status(400).json({
00276:       ok: false,
00277:       error: "invalid_lottery",
00278:     });
00279:   }
00280: 
00281:   const scanLimit = req.query.scanLimit
00282:     ? Number(req.query.scanLimit)
00283:     : undefined;
00284: 
00285:   const edgeLimit = req.query.edgeLimit
00286:     ? Number(req.query.edgeLimit)
00287:     : undefined;
00288: 
00289:   try {
00290:     const db = getDb();
00291: 
00292:     const { lk, result, warn } = await computeBounds(db, lottery, {
00293:       scanLimit,
00294:       edgeLimit,
00295:     });
00296: 
00297:     return res.json({
00298:       ok: true,
00299:       lottery: lk,
00300:       minYmd: result?.minYmd || null,
00301:       maxYmd: result?.maxYmd || null,
00302:       source: result?.source || "unknown",
00303:       minDocId: result?.minDocId || null,
00304:       maxDocId: result?.maxDocId || null,
00305:       sampleCount: result?.sampleCount || null,
00306:       firstDocId: result?.firstDocId || null,
00307:       lastDocId: result?.lastDocId || null,
00308:       note:
00309:         warn && isIndexError(warn)
00310:           ? "faltou índice composto em uma tentativa (ok, caiu no fallback)"
00311:           : null,
00312:     });
00313:   } catch (e) {
00314:     return res.status(500).json({
00315:       ok: false,
00316:       error: "internal_error",
00317:       message: String(e?.message || e || "erro"),
00318:     });
00319:   }
00320: });

00013: function normalizeLotteryKey(v) {
00014:   const s = String(v ?? "").trim().toUpperCase();
00015: 
00016:   if (s === "RJ" || s === "RIO" || s === "PT-RIO" || s === "PT_RIO") return "PT_RIO";
00017:   if (s === "FED" || s === "FEDERAL" || s === "BR") return "FEDERAL";
00018: 
00019:   return "";
00020: }

00025: function getExpectedHours(lottery, date, hasFederal) {
00026:   if (lottery !== "PT_RIO") return [];
00027: 
00028:   const dt = new Date(`${date}T12:00:00`);
00029:   const dow = dt.getDay(); // 0 = domingo
00030: 
00031:   // Domingo: só 09, 11, 14, 16
00032:   if (dow === 0) {
00033:     return ["09:00", "11:00", "14:00", "16:00"];
00034:   }
00035: 
00036:   // Base fixa todos os outros dias
00037:   const expected = ["09:00", "11:00", "14:00", "16:00"];
00038: 
00039:   // 21h existe fora domingo
00040:   expected.push("21:00");
00041: 
00042:   // 18h só se NÃO tiver Federal no dia
00043:   if (!hasFederal) {
00044:     expected.push("18:00");
00045:   }
00046: 
00047:   return expected.sort();
00048: }

00050: function checkDrawIntegrity(draws, lottery, date, hasFederal) {
00051:   const expectedHours = getExpectedHours(lottery, date, hasFederal);
00052: 
00053:   if (!expectedHours.length) {
00054:     return {
00055:       ok: true,
00056:       missing: [],
00057:       extra: [],
00058:       expectedHours: [],
00059:       hasFederal: Boolean(hasFederal),
00060:     };
00061:   }
00062: 
00063:   const gotHours = (Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [])
00064:     .map((d) => String(d?.close_hour || "").trim())
00065:     .filter(Boolean);
00066: 
00067:   const missing = expectedHours.filter((h) => !gotHours.includes(h));
00068:   const extra = gotHours.filter((h) => !expectedHours.includes(h));
00069: 
00070:   if (missing.length || extra.length) {
00071:     console.warn(
00072:       `[DRAW ALERT] ${lottery} ${date} missing=${missing.join(", ")} extra=${extra.join(", ")} hasFederal=${hasFederal ? "1" : "0"}`
00073:     );
00074:   }
00075: 
00076:   return {
00077:     ok: missing.length === 0 && extra.length === 0,
00078:     missing,
00079:     extra,
00080:     expectedHours,
00081:     hasFederal: Boolean(hasFederal),
00082:   };
00083: }

00088: function isISODate(s) {
00089:   const str = String(s || "").trim();
00090:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(str)) return false;
00091: 
00092:   const [y, m, d] = str.split("-").map(Number);
00093:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
00094: 
00095:   return (
00096:     dt.getUTCFullYear() === y &&
00097:     dt.getUTCMonth() === m - 1 &&
00098:     dt.getUTCDate() === d
00099:   );
00100: }
00101: 
00102: function isHHMM(s) {
00103:   const str = String(s || "").trim();
00104:   const m = str.match(/^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
00105:   if (!m) return false;
00106: 
00107:   const hh = Number(m[1]);
00108:   const mm = Number(m[2]);
00109: 
00110:   return hh >= 0 && hh <= 23 && mm >= 0 && mm <= 59;
00111: }
00112: 
00113: function normalizeHHMM(value) {
00114:   const s = String(value ?? "").trim();
00115:   if (!s) return "";
00116: 
00117:   if (isHHMM(s)) return s;
00118: 
00119:   const m1 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})h$/i);
00120:   if (m1) {
00121:     const hh = Number(m1[1]);
00122:     if (hh >= 0 && hh <= 23) return `${String(hh).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
00123:     return "";
00124:   }
00125: 
00126:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
00127:   if (m2) {
00128:     const hh = Number(m2[1]);
00129:     if (hh >= 0 && hh <= 23) return `${String(hh).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
00130:     return "";
00131:   }
00132: 
00133:   const m3 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})$/);
00134:   if (m3) {
00135:     const hh = Number(m3[1]);
00136:     const mm = Number(m3[2]);
00137:     if (hh >= 0 && hh <= 23 && mm >= 0 && mm <= 59) {
00138:       return `${String(hh).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(mm).padStart(2, "0")}`;
00139:     }
00140:     return "";
00141:   }
00142: 
00143:   return "";
00144: }
00145: 
00146: function cmpHHMM(a, b) {
00147:   return String(a || "").localeCompare(String(b || ""));
00148: }
00149: 
00150: function inRangeHHMM(hhmm, from, to) {
00151:   const h = String(hhmm || "").trim();
00152:   if (!h || !isHHMM(h)) return false;
00153:   if (from && cmpHHMM(h, from) < 0) return false;
00154:   if (to && cmpHHMM(h, to) > 0) return false;
00155:   return true;
00156: }

00173:   const toRaw = req.query.to != null ? String(req.query.to).trim() : "";

00188: function parsePositionsParam(v) {
00189:   const raw = String(v ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
00190:   if (!raw) return { positions: null, maxPos: null };
00191: 
00192:   const m = raw.match(/^(\d+)\s*(?:\-|\.\.)\s*(\d+)$/);
00193:   if (m) {
00194:     const a = Math.max(1, Number(m[1]));
00195:     const b = Math.max(1, Number(m[2]));
00196:     const lo = Math.min(a, b);
00197:     const hi = Math.max(a, b);
00198:     const positions = [];
00199:     for (let i = lo; i <= hi; i += 1) positions.push(i);
00200:     return { positions, maxPos: hi };
00201:   }
00202: 
00203:   const parts = raw
00204:     .split(",")
00205:     .map((s) => Number(String(s).trim()))
00206:     .filter((n) => Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 1);
00207: 
00208:   if (!parts.length) return { positions: null, maxPos: null };
00209: 
00210:   const uniq = Array.from(new Set(parts)).sort((a, b) => a - b);
00211:   return { positions: uniq, maxPos: uniq[uniq.length - 1] };
00212: }

00231: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limitN, mapper) {
00232:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
00233:   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 6);
00234:   const results = new Array(arr.length);
00235:   let idx = 0;
00236: 
00237:   async function worker() {
00238:     while (true) {
00239:       const current = idx;
00240:       idx += 1;
00241:       if (current >= arr.length) break;
00242:       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
00243:     }
00244:   }
00245: 
00246:   await Promise.all(
00247:     Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, () => worker())
00248:   );
00249: 
00250:   return results;
00251: }

00253: async function loadPrizesForDraws(db, drawsWindow, includePrizes, positionsInfo) {
00254:   if (!includePrizes) return drawsWindow;

00256:   const posArr =
00257:     positionsInfo && Array.isArray(positionsInfo.positions)
00258:       ? positionsInfo.positions

00260: 
00261:   const posSet =
00262:     posArr && posArr.length
00263:       ? new Set(posArr.map((n) => Number(n)))
00264:       : null;
00265: 
00266:   const draws = await mapWithConcurrency(drawsWindow, 6, async (d) => {
00267:     let q = db
00268:       .collection("draws")
00269:       .doc(d.id)
00270:       .collection("prizes")
00271:       .orderBy("position", "asc");

00274:       const maxPos = Number(positionsInfo.maxPos) || null;
00275:       const contiguousFrom1 =
00276:         maxPos &&
00277:         posArr.length === maxPos &&
00278:         Number(posArr[0]) === 1 &&
00279:         Number(posArr[posArr.length - 1]) === maxPos;
00280: 
00281:       if (contiguousFrom1) q = q.limit(maxPos);
00282:     }

00287:       ? rows.filter((r) => posSet.has(Number(r.position)))

00290:     return { ...d, prizes };

00403:     const hasFederal = hasFederalInDraws(rawDraws);
00404: 
00405:     const byLottery = applyLotteryFilter(rawDraws, lottery).sort(sortDrawsByHourOnly);
00406:     const drawsWindow = applyHourWindow(byLottery, from || "", to || "");
00407:     const draws = await loadPrizesForDraws(db, drawsWindow, includePrizes, positionsInfo);
00408: 
00409:     const integrity = checkDrawIntegrity(draws, lottery, date, hasFederal);
00410: 
00411:     return res.json({
00412:       ok: true,
00413:       integrity,
00414:       date,
00415:       lottery,
00416:       from: from || null,
00417:       to: to || null,
00418:       includePrizes,
00419:       count: draws.length,
00420:       draws,
00421:     });
00422:   } catch (e) {
00423:     return res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "erro" });
00424:   }
00425: });

00433: async function handleDay(req, res) {
00434:   try {
00435:     const db = getDb();
00436: 
00437:     const date = String(req.query.date || "").trim();
00438:     const lottery = getLotteryFromQuery(req);
00439: 
00440:     if (!requireLotteryOr400(lottery, res)) return;
00441: 
00442:     if (!isISODate(date)) {
00443:       return res.status(400).json({
00444:         ok: false,
00445:         error: "Parâmetro inválido: date (use YYYY-MM-DD)",
00446:       });
00447:     }
00448: 
00449:     const win = getWindowFromQuery(req);
00450:     if (win.error) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: win.error });
00451: 
00452:     const includePrizes = parseIncludePrizes(req.query.includePrizes, true);
00453:     const positionsInfo = parsePositionsParam(req.query.positions);
00454: 
00455:     const docs = await fetchDayDraws(db, date);
00456:     const rawDraws = docs.map(normalizeDrawDoc);
00457:     const hasFederal = hasFederalInDraws(rawDraws);
00458: 
00459:     const byLottery = applyLotteryFilter(rawDraws, lottery).sort(sortDrawsByHourOnly);
00460:     const drawsWindow = applyHourWindow(byLottery, win.from || "", win.to || "");
00461:     const draws = await loadPrizesForDraws(db, drawsWindow, includePrizes, positionsInfo);
00462: 
00463:     const integrity = checkDrawIntegrity(draws, lottery, date, hasFederal);
00464: 
00465:     return res.json({
00466:       ok: true,
00467:       mode: "day",
00468:       integrity,
00469:       date,
00470:       lottery,
00471:       from: win.from || null,
00472:       to: win.to || null,
00473:       includePrizes,
00474:       count: draws.length,
00475:       draws,
00476:     });
00477:   } catch (e) {
00478:     return res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "erro" });
00479:   }
00480: }
00481: 
00482: async function handleRange(req, res) {
00483:   try {
00484:     const db = getDb();
00485: 
00486:     const dateFrom = String(req.query.dateFrom || "").trim();
00487:     const dateTo = String(req.query.dateTo || "").trim();
00488:     const lottery = getLotteryFromQuery(req);
00489: 
00490:     if (!requireLotteryOr400(lottery, res)) return;
00491: 
00492:     if (!isISODate(dateFrom) || !isISODate(dateTo)) {
00493:       return res.status(400).json({
00494:         ok: false,
00495:         error: "Parâmetro inválido: dateFrom/dateTo (use YYYY-MM-DD)",
00496:       });
00497:     }
00498: 
00499:     if (dateFrom > dateTo) {
00500:       return res.status(400).json({
00501:         ok: false,
00502:         error: "Parâmetro inválido: dateFrom não pode ser maior que dateTo",
00503:       });
00504:     }
00505: 
00506:     const win = getWindowFromQuery(req);
00507:     if (win.error) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: win.error });
00508: 
00509:     const includePrizes = parseIncludePrizes(req.query.includePrizes, false);
00510:     const positionsInfo = parsePositionsParam(req.query.positions);
00511: 
00512:     const snap = await db
00513:       .collection("draws")
00514:       .where("ymd", ">=", dateFrom)
00515:       .where("ymd", "<=", dateTo)
00516:       .orderBy("ymd", "asc")
00517:       .get();
00518: 
00519:     const rawDraws = snap.docs.map(normalizeDrawDoc);
00520:     const byLottery = applyLotteryFilter(rawDraws, lottery).sort(sortDrawsByDateThenHour);
00521:     const drawsWindow = applyHourWindow(byLottery, win.from || "", win.to || "");
00522:     const draws = await loadPrizesForDraws(db, drawsWindow, includePrizes, positionsInfo);
00523: 
00524:     const groupedIntegrity = {};
00525:     if (lottery === "PT_RIO") {
00526:       const rawByDay = rawDraws.reduce((acc, d) => {
00527:         const ymd = String(d.ymd || d.date || "").trim();
00528:         if (!ymd) return acc;
00529:         if (!acc[ymd]) acc[ymd] = [];
00530:         acc[ymd].push(d);
00531:         return acc;
00532:       }, {});
00533: 
00534:       const filteredByDay = draws.reduce((acc, d) => {
00535:         const ymd = String(d.ymd || d.date || "").trim();
00536:         if (!ymd) return acc;
00537:         if (!acc[ymd]) acc[ymd] = [];
00538:         acc[ymd].push(d);
00539:         return acc;
00540:       }, {});
00541: 
00542:       const allDays = Array.from(
00543:         new Set([
00544:           ...Object.keys(rawByDay),
00545:           ...Object.keys(filteredByDay),
00546:         ])
00547:       ).sort();
00548: 
00549:       for (const ymd of allDays) {
00550:         const dayRaw = rawByDay[ymd] || [];
00551:         const dayFiltered = filteredByDay[ymd] || [];
00552:         const hasFederal = hasFederalInDraws(dayRaw);
00553:         groupedIntegrity[ymd] = checkDrawIntegrity(dayFiltered, lottery, ymd, hasFederal);
00554:       }
00555:     }
00556: 
00557:     return res.json({
00558:       ok: true,
00559:       mode: "range",
00560:       integrityByDay: groupedIntegrity,
00561:       dateFrom,
00562:       dateTo,
00563:       lottery,
00564:       from: win.from || null,
00565:       to: win.to || null,
00566:       includePrizes,
00567:       count: draws.length,
00568:       draws,
00569:     });
00570:   } catch (e) {
00571:     return res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "erro" });
00572:   }
00573: }
00574: 
00575: router.get("/draws/day", handleDay);
00576: router.get("/draws/range", handleRange);
00577:

00016: function todayYMDInSaoPaulo() {
00017:   try {
00018:     const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", {
00019:       timeZone: "America/Sao_Paulo",
00020:       year: "numeric",
00021:       month: "2-digit",
00022:       day: "2-digit",
00023:     }).formatToParts(new Date());
00024:     const y = parts.find((p) => p.type === "year")?.value || "1970";
00025:     const m = parts.find((p) => p.type === "month")?.value || "01";
00026:     const d = parts.find((p) => p.type === "day")?.value || "01";
00027:     return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
00028:   } catch {
00029:     const d = new Date();
00030:     const y = d.getFullYear();
00031:     const m = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
00032:     const dd = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
00033:     return `${y}-${m}-${dd}`;
00034:   }
00035: }

00037: function nowMinutesInSaoPaulo() {
00038:   try {
00039:     const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", {
00040:       timeZone: "America/Sao_Paulo",
00041:       hour: "2-digit",
00042:       minute: "2-digit",
00043:       hour12: false,
00044:     }).formatToParts(new Date());

00049:     return hh * 60 + mm;

00095: function isISODate(s) {
00096:   const str = String(s || "").trim();
00097:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(str)) return false;
00098: 
00099:   const [y, m, d] = str.split("-").map(Number);
00100:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
00101: 
00102:   return (
00103:     dt.getUTCFullYear() === y &&
00104:     dt.getUTCMonth() === m - 1 &&
00105:     dt.getUTCDate() === d
00106:   );
00107: }

00109: function isHHMM(s) {
00110:   const str = String(s || "").trim();
00111:   const m = str.match(/^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
00112:   if (!m) return false;
00113: 
00114:   const hh = Number(m[1]);
00115:   const mm = Number(m[2]);
00116: 
00117:   return hh >= 0 && hh <= 23 && mm >= 0 && mm <= 59;
00118: }

00124: function normalizeHHMM(value) {
00125:   const s = String(value ?? "").trim();
00126:   if (!s) return "";
00127: 
00128:   if (isHHMM(s)) return s;
00129: 
00130:   const m1 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})h$/i);
00131:   if (m1) {
00132:     const hh = Number(m1[1]);
00133:     if (hh >= 0 && hh <= 23) return `${pad2(hh)}:00`;
00134:     return "";
00135:   }
00136: 
00137:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
00138:   if (m2) {
00139:     const hh = Number(m2[1]);
00140:     if (hh >= 0 && hh <= 23) return `${pad2(hh)}:00`;
00141:     return "";
00142:   }
00143: 
00144:   const m3 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})$/);
00145:   if (m3) {
00146:     const hh = Number(m3[1]);
00147:     const mm = Number(m3[2]);
00148:     if (hh >= 0 && hh <= 23 && mm >= 0 && mm <= 59) {
00149:       return `${pad2(hh)}:${pad2(mm)}`;
00150:     }
00151:     return "";
00152:   }
00153: 
00154:   return "";
00155: }

00330: function normalizeHourList(arr) {
00331:   const out = [];

00334:     const m = String(v ?? "").match(/\d{1,2}/);
00335:     if (!m) continue;

00340:       seen.add(hh);

00376: function pickRangeForDate(ranges, ymd) {
00377:   for (const r of Array.isArray(ranges) ? ranges : []) {
00378:     const from0 = String(r?.from || "").trim();
00379:     const to0 = String(r?.to || "").trim();
00380: 
00381:     const hasFrom = isISODate(from0);
00382:     const hasTo = isISODate(to0);
00383: 
00384:     if (!hasFrom && !hasTo) return r;
00385: 
00386:     if (!hasFrom && hasTo) {
00387:       if (ymd <= to0) return r;
00388:       continue;
00389:     }
00390:     if (hasFrom && !hasTo) {
00391:       if (ymd >= from0) return r;
00392:       continue;
00393:     }
00394: 
00395:     if (hasFrom && hasTo && ymd >= from0 && ymd <= to0) return r;
00396:   }
00397:   return null;
00398: }

00409: function getExpectedForDate(lotteryKey, ymd, include09FromYmdDefault) {

00411:   const scheduleRaw = safeReadJson(p, null);
00412:   const scheduleFile = !!scheduleRaw;
00413: 
00414:   if (scheduleRaw) {
00415:     const global = parseScheduleGlobal(scheduleRaw);
00416:     if (global && (global.hard.length || global.soft.length)) return global;
00417:   }
00418: 
00419:   const ranges = Array.isArray(scheduleRaw) ? scheduleRaw : scheduleRaw?.ranges;
00420:   const r = pickRangeForDate(ranges, ymd);
00421: 
00422:   if (r) {
00423:     if (r.dow && typeof r.dow === "object") {
00424:       const d = new Date(String(ymd) + "T12:00:00-03:00");
00425:       const dow = String(d.getDay());
00426:       const block = r.dow[dow] || r.dow[Number(dow)] || null;
00427: 
00428:       const hard = normalizeHourList(block?.hard || block?.expectedHard || []);
00429:       const soft = normalizeHourList(block?.soft || block?.expectedSoft || []);
00430:       const hours = normalizeHourList(block?.hours || []);
00431: 
00432:       if (hours.length) return { hard: hours, soft: [], mode: "schedule", scheduleFile: true };
00433:       return { hard, soft, mode: "schedule", scheduleFile: true };
00434:     }
00435: 
00436:     const hard = normalizeHourList(r?.hard || r?.expectedHard || []);
00437:     const soft = normalizeHourList(r?.soft || r?.expectedSoft || []);
00438:     const hours = normalizeHourList(r?.hours || []);
00439:     if (hours.length) return { hard: hours, soft: [], mode: "schedule", scheduleFile: true };
00440:     return { hard, soft, mode: "schedule", scheduleFile: true };
00441:   }
00442: 
00443:   if (lotteryKey === "PT_RIO") {
00444:     return expectedHoursPT_RIO_FALLBACK(ymd, include09FromYmdDefault || "2024-01-05");
00445:   }
00446: 
00447:   return { hard: [], soft: [], mode: "none", scheduleFile };
00448: }
00449: 
00450: /* =========================
00451:    SOURCE GAPS
00452: ========================= */
00453: 
00454: const _gapsCache = new Map();
00455: const GAPS_TTL_MS = 60_000;
00456: 
00457: function readGapsMap(lottery, opts) {
00458:   const key = upTrim(lottery || "PT_RIO") || "PT_RIO";
00459:   const reload = !!(opts && opts.reload);
00460: 
00461:   const cached = _gapsCache.get(key);
00462:   if (!reload && cached && Date.now() - cached.loadedAt < GAPS_TTL_MS) {
00463:     return cached.map;
00464:   }
00465: 
00466:   const p = path.join(DEFAULT_GAPS_DIR, `${key}.json`);
00467:   let map = {};
00468:   try {
00469:     if (fs.existsSync(p)) {
00470:       const raw = fs.readFileSync(p, "utf8");
00471:       const j = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : {};
00472:       if (j && typeof j === "object") map = j;
00473:     }
00474:   } catch {
00475:     map = {};
00476:   }
00477: 
00478:   _gapsCache.set(key, { loadedAt: Date.now(), map });
00479:   return map;
00480: }
00481: 
00482: function normalizeGapEntry(entry) {
00483:   if (Array.isArray(entry)) {
00484:     return {
00485:       removedHard: normalizeHourList(entry),
00486:       removedSoft: [],
00487:     };
00488:   }
00489: 
00490:   const obj = entry && typeof entry === "object" ? entry : {};
00491:   const hard = obj.removedHard || obj.removeHard || obj.hard || obj.expectedRemovedHard || [];
00492:   const soft = obj.removedSoft || obj.removeSoft || obj.soft || obj.expectedRemovedSoft || [];
00493: 
00494:   return {
00495:     removedHard: normalizeHourList(hard),
00496:     removedSoft: normalizeHourList(soft),
00497:   };
00498: }
00499: 
00500: function getGapsForDate(lotteryKey, ymd, opts) {
00501:   const map = readGapsMap(lotteryKey, opts);
00502:   const entry = map?.gapsByDay?.[ymd] ?? map?.[ymd];
00503: 
00504:   if (!entry) return { removedHard: [], removedSoft: [] };
00505:   return normalizeGapEntry(entry);
00506: }
00507: 
00508: /* =========================
00509:    CONCURRENCY
00510: ========================= */
00511: 
00512: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limitN, mapper) {
00513:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
00514:   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 6);
00515:   const results = new Array(arr.length);
00516:   let idx = 0;
00517: 
00518:   async function worker() {
00519:     while (true) {
00520:       const current = idx;
00521:       idx += 1;
00522:       if (current >= arr.length) break;
00523:       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
00524:     }
00525:   }
00526: 
00527:   await Promise.all(
00528:     Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, () => worker())
00529:   );
00530:   return results;
00531: }
00532: 
00533: /* =========================
00534:    ROUTE
00535: ========================= */
00536: 
00537: function buildCalendarStatus({ slotsSummary, blocked = false, blockedReason = "", isToday = false }) {
00538:   const expected = Number(slotsSummary?.expectedTotal || 0);
00539:   const found = Number(slotsSummary?.foundTotal || 0);
00540:   const missing = Number(slotsSummary?.missingTotal || 0);
00541: 
00542:   let status = "empty";
00543:   if (blocked) status = blockedReason === "future_date" ? "future" : "blocked";
00544:   else if (isToday && missing > 0) status = "pending";
00545:   else if (expected > 0 && missing === 0) status = "complete";
00546:   else if (found > 0 && missing > 0) status = "partial";
00547:   else if (expected > 0 && found === 0) status = "empty";
00548: 
00549:   return {
00550:     status,
00551:     expected,
00552:     found,
00553:     missing,
00554:     completionPct: Number(slotsSummary?.completionPct ?? 0),
00555:   };
00556: }
00557: 
00558: function buildSlots(opts) {
00559:   const {
00560:     scheduleAll,
00561:     byHour,
00562:     baseSoft,
00563:     removedHardApplied,
00564:     removedSoftApplied,
00565:     expectedHard,
00566:     expectedSoft,
00567:     expectedHardPublished,
00568:     expectedSoftPublished,
00569:     capToday,
00570:     isToday,
00571:     nowMinBR,
00572:     slotGraceMin,
00573:   } = opts || {};
00574: 
00575:   return (Array.isArray(scheduleAll) ? scheduleAll : []).map((hh) => {
00576:     const draw = byHour?.get ? byHour.get(hh) || null : null;
00577: 
00578:     const isSoft = Array.isArray(baseSoft) && baseSoft.includes(hh);
00579:     const kind = isSoft ? "soft" : "hard";
00580: 
00581:     if (removedHardApplied?.has?.(hh) || removedSoftApplied?.has?.(hh)) {
00582:       return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "source_gap", draw: null };
00583:     }
00584: 
00585:     if (draw) return { hour: hh, kind, status: "valid", draw };
00586: 
00587:     if (
00588:       capToday &&
00589:       isToday &&
00590:       ((expectedHard && expectedHard.includes(hh)) || (expectedSoft && expectedSoft.includes(hh)))
00591:     ) {
00592:       const published = isSlotPublishedToday(hh, nowMinBR, slotGraceMin);
00593:       if (!published) {
00594:         return { hour: hh, kind, status: "future", reason: "not_yet_published", draw: null };
00595:       }
00596:     }
00597: 
00598:     if (expectedHardPublished && expectedHardPublished.includes(hh)) {
00599:       return { hour: hh, kind: "hard", status: "missing", draw: null };
00600:     }
00601:     if (expectedSoftPublished && expectedSoftPublished.includes(hh)) {
00602:       return { hour: hh, kind: "soft", status: "soft_missing", draw: null };
00603:     }
00604: 
00605:     return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "not_expected", draw: null };
00606:   });
00607: }

00015: function isISODate(s) {
00016:   const str = String(s || "").trim();
00017:   const m = str.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
00018:   if (!m) return false;
00019: 
00020:   const y = Number(m[1]);
00021:   const mo = Number(m[2]);
00022:   const d = Number(m[3]);
00023: 
00024:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, mo - 1, d));
00025:   return (

00027:     dt.getUTCMonth() === mo - 1 &&
00028:     dt.getUTCDate() === d

00032: function isHHMM(s) {
00033:   const str = String(s || "").trim();
00034:   const m = str.match(/^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
00035:   if (!m) return false;
00036: 
00037:   const hh = Number(m[1]);
00038:   const mm = Number(m[2]);
00039: 
00040:   return hh >= 0 && hh <= 23 && mm >= 0 && mm <= 59;
00041: }
00042: 
00043: // aceita "21", "21h", "21:0", "21:00"
00044: function normalizeHHMM(value) {
00045:   const s = String(value ?? "").trim();
00046:   if (!s) return "";
00047: 
00048:   if (isHHMM(s)) return s;
00049: 
00050:   const m1 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})h$/i);
00051:   if (m1) {
00052:     const hh = Number(m1[1]);
00053:     if (hh >= 0 && hh <= 23) return `${pad2(hh)}:00`;
00054:     return "";
00055:   }
00056: 
00057:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
00058:   if (m2) {
00059:     const hh = Number(m2[1]);
00060:     if (hh >= 0 && hh <= 23) return `${pad2(hh)}:00`;
00061:     return "";
00062:   }
00063: 
00064:   const m3 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})$/);
00065:   if (m3) {
00066:     const hh = Number(m3[1]);
00067:     const mm = Number(m3[2]);
00068:     if (hh >= 0 && hh <= 23 && mm >= 0 && mm <= 59) {
00069:       return `${pad2(hh)}:${pad2(mm)}`;
00070:     }
00071:     return "";
00072:   }
00073: 
00074:   return "";
00075: }

00005: function pad2(n) {
00006:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");

00009: function isISODate(s) {
00010:   const str = String(s || "").trim();
00011:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(str)) return false;
00012: 
00013:   const [y, m, d] = str.split("-").map(Number);
00014:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
00015: 
00016:   return (
00017:     dt.getUTCFullYear() === y &&
00018:     dt.getUTCMonth() === m - 1 &&
00019:     dt.getUTCDate() === d
00020:   );
00021: }

00023: function ymdInTimeZone(date, timeZone = "America/Sao_Paulo") {
00024:   if (!(date instanceof Date) || Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return null;
00025: 
00026:   try {
00027:     const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", {
00028:       timeZone,
00029:       year: "numeric",
00030:       month: "2-digit",
00031:       day: "2-digit",
00032:     }).formatToParts(date);
00033: 
00034:     const y = parts.find((p) => p.type === "year")?.value;
00035:     const m = parts.find((p) => p.type === "month")?.value;
00036:     const d = parts.find((p) => p.type === "day")?.value;
00037: 
00038:     if (!y || !m || !d) return null;
00039:     const out = `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
00040:     return isISODate(out) ? out : null;
00041:   } catch {
00042:     // fallback local timezone do servidor (menos ideal)
00043:     const y = date.getFullYear();
00044:     const m = pad2(date.getMonth() + 1);
00045:     const d = pad2(date.getDate());
00046:     const out = `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
00047:     return isISODate(out) ? out : null;
00048:   }
00049: }
00050: 
00051: function normalizeToYMD(input) {
00052:   if (input == null) return null;
00053: 
00054:   // Firestore Timestamp (admin) / FieldValue com toDate()
00055:   if (typeof input === "object" && typeof input.toDate === "function") {
00056:     const d = input.toDate();
00057:     return ymdInTimeZone(d, "America/Sao_Paulo");
00058:   }
00059: 
00060:   // Timestamp-like {seconds} ou {_seconds}
00061:   if (
00062:     typeof input === "object" &&
00063:     (Number.isFinite(Number(input.seconds)) ||
00064:       Number.isFinite(Number(input._seconds)))
00065:   ) {
00066:     const sec = Number.isFinite(Number(input.seconds))
00067:       ? Number(input.seconds)
00068:       : Number(input._seconds);
00069: 
00070:     const d = new Date(sec * 1000);
00071:     return ymdInTimeZone(d, "America/Sao_Paulo");
00072:   }
00073: 
00074:   // Date
00075:   if (input instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(input.getTime())) {
00076:     return ymdInTimeZone(input, "America/Sao_Paulo");
00077:   }
00078: 
00079:   // String
00080:   const s = String(input || "").trim();
00081:   if (!s) return null;
00082: 
00083:   // ISO (aceita com hora, mas valida o dia)
00084:   const iso = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/);
00085:   if (iso) {
00086:     const out = `${iso[1]}-${iso[2]}-${iso[3]}`;
00087:     return isISODate(out) ? out : null;
00088:   }
00089: 
00090:   // BR dd/mm/yyyy
00091:   const br = s.match(/^(\d{2})\/(\d{2})\/(\d{4})$/);
00092:   if (br) {
00093:     const out = `${br[3]}-${br[2]}-${br[1]}`;
00094:     return isISODate(out) ? out : null;
00095:   }
00096: 
00097:   return null;
00098: }

00100: function pickMinMaxFromDocs(docs) {
00101:   let minYmd = null;
00102:   let maxYmd = null;
00103: 
00104:   for (const doc of docs || []) {
00105:     const d = doc?.data ? doc.data() : doc || {};
00106: 
00107:     const y =
00108:       d?.ymd ||
00109:       normalizeToYMD(
00110:         d?.date ?? d?.data ?? d?.dt ?? d?.draw_date ?? d?.close_date
00111:       );
00112: 
00113:     if (!y || !isISODate(y)) continue;
00114: 
00115:     if (!minYmd || y < minYmd) minYmd = y;
00116:     if (!maxYmd || y > maxYmd) maxYmd = y;
00117:   }
00118: 
00119:   return { minYmd, maxYmd };
00120: }

00133: async function tryBoundsByField(db, lotteryKey, field, scanLimit = 50) {
00134:   const DOC_ID = admin.firestore.FieldPath.documentId();
00135:   const base = db.collection("draws").where("lottery_key", "==", lotteryKey);
00136: 
00137:   const ascSnap = await base
00138:     .orderBy(field, "asc")
00139:     .orderBy(DOC_ID, "asc")
00140:     .limit(scanLimit)
00141:     .get();
00142: 
00143:   const descSnap = await base
00144:     .orderBy(field, "desc")
00145:     .orderBy(DOC_ID, "desc")
00146:     .limit(scanLimit)
00147:     .get();
00148: 
00149:   if (ascSnap.empty || descSnap.empty) {
00150:     return {
00151:       ok: false,
00152:       source: `where(lottery_key)+orderBy(${field})+docId(scan=${scanLimit})`,
00153:     };
00154:   }
00155: 
00156:   function pickFirstValidYmdFromDocs(docs, fieldName) {
00157:     for (const doc of docs || []) {
00158:       const v = doc?.data()?.[fieldName];
00159:       const y = normalizeToYMD(v);
00160:       if (y && isISODate(y)) {
00161:         return { ymd: y, docId: doc.id, raw: v };
00162:       }
00163:     }
00164:     return { ymd: null, docId: null, raw: null };
00165:   }
00166: 
00167:   const minPick = pickFirstValidYmdFromDocs(ascSnap.docs, field);
00168:   const maxPick = pickFirstValidYmdFromDocs(descSnap.docs, field);
00169: 
00170:   return {
00171:     ok: !!(minPick.ymd && maxPick.ymd),
00172:     minYmd: minPick.ymd,
00173:     maxYmd: maxPick.ymd,
00174:     source: `where(lottery_key)+orderBy(${field})+docId(scan=${scanLimit})`,
00175:     minDocId: minPick.docId,
00176:     maxDocId: maxPick.docId,
00177:     minRaw: minPick.raw ?? null,
00178:     maxRaw: maxPick.raw ?? null,
00179:   };
00180: }

00182: async function fallbackBoundsByDocIdEdges(db, lotteryKey, edgeLimit) {
00183:   const DOC_ID = admin.firestore.FieldPath.documentId();
00184:   const base = db.collection("draws").where("lottery_key", "==", lotteryKey);

00193:     ok: !!(minYmd && maxYmd),

00195:     maxYmd: maxYmd || null,

00199:     lastDocId: descSnap.docs[descSnap.docs.length - 1]?.id || null,

00009: function addDays(ymd, n) {
00010:   const d = new Date(ymd + "T00:00:00Z");
00011:   d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() + n);
00012:   return d.toISOString().slice(0,10);

00016:   return new Date(ymd+"T00:00:00Z").getUTCDay();
00017: }
00018: 
00019: function expectedHours(ymd){
00020: 
00021:   const dow = weekday(ymd);
00022: 
00023:   // domingo
00024:   if(dow===0)
00025:     return ["09:00","11:00","14:00","16:00"];
00026: 
00027:   // quarta
00028:   if(dow===3)
00029:     return ["09:00","11:00","14:00","16:00","21:00"];
00030: 
00031:   // sábado
00032:   if(dow===6)
00033:     return ["09:00","11:00","14:00","16:00","21:00"];
00034: 
00035:   return ["09:00","11:00","14:00","16:00","18:00","21:00"];
00036: }

00038: function normHour(v){
00039:   const m=String(v||"").match(/(\d{1,2})/);
00040:   if(!m) return "";
00041:   return String(Number(m[1])).padStart(2,"0")+":00";
00042: }

00005: function pad2(n) {
00006:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
00007: }
00008: 
00009: function isISODate(s) {
00010:   const str = String(s || "").trim();
00011:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(str)) return false;
00012: 
00013:   const [y, m, d] = str.split("-").map(Number);
00014:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
00015: 
00016:   return (
00017:     dt.getUTCFullYear() === y &&
00018:     dt.getUTCMonth() === m - 1 &&
00019:     dt.getUTCDate() === d
00020:   );
00021: }
00022: 
00023: function normalizeToYMD(input) {
00024:   if (!input) return null;
00025: 
00026:   // Timestamp (admin)
00027:   if (typeof input === "object" && typeof input.toDate === "function") {
00028:     const d = input.toDate();
00029:     if (!Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) {
00030:       return `${d.getUTCFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getUTCMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(
00031:         d.getUTCDate()
00032:       )}`;
00033:     }
00034:   }
00035: 
00036:   // Timestamp-like { seconds } / { _seconds }
00037:   if (
00038:     typeof input === "object" &&
00039:     (Number.isFinite(Number(input.seconds)) ||
00040:       Number.isFinite(Number(input._seconds)))
00041:   ) {
00042:     const sec = Number.isFinite(Number(input.seconds))
00043:       ? Number(input.seconds)
00044:       : Number(input._seconds);
00045:     const d = new Date(sec * 1000);
00046:     if (!Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) {
00047:       return `${d.getUTCFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getUTCMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(
00048:         d.getUTCDate()
00049:       )}`;
00050:     }
00051:   }
00052: 
00053:   if (input instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(input.getTime())) {
00054:     return `${input.getUTCFullYear()}-${pad2(input.getUTCMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(
00055:       input.getUTCDate()
00056:     )}`;
00057:   }
00058: 
00059:   const s = String(input || "").trim();
00060:   if (!s) return null;
00061: 
00062:   // ISO
00063:   const iso = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/);
00064:   if (iso) return `${iso[1]}-${iso[2]}-${iso[3]}`;
00065: 
00066:   // BR
00067:   const br = s.match(/^(\d{2})\/(\d{2})\/(\d{4})$/);
00068:   if (br) return `${br[3]}-${br[2]}-${br[1]}`;
00069: 
00070:   return null;
00071: }
00072: 
00073: function pickMinMaxFromDocs(docs) {
00074:   let minYmd = null;
00075:   let maxYmd = null;
00076: 
00077:   for (const doc of docs || []) {
00078:     const d = doc?.data ? doc.data() : doc || {};
00079: 
00080:     const y =
00081:       d?.ymd ||
00082:       normalizeToYMD(
00083:         d?.date ?? d?.data ?? d?.dt ?? d?.draw_date ?? d?.close_date
00084:       );
00085: 
00086:     if (!y || !isISODate(y)) continue;
00087: 
00088:     if (!minYmd || y < minYmd) minYmd = y;
00089:     if (!maxYmd || y > maxYmd) maxYmd = y;
00090:   }
00091: 
00092:   return { minYmd, maxYmd };
00093: }

00095: function isIndexError(err) {
00096:   const msg = String(err?.message || "").toLowerCase();
00097:   const code = String(err?.code || "").toLowerCase();
00098:   return (
00099:     code.includes("failed-precondition") ||
00100:     msg.includes("failed_precondition") ||
00101:     (msg.includes("index") && msg.includes("create")) ||

00106: /**
00107:  * ✅ FIX: bounds por campo com SCAN curto (evita pegar 1 doc inválido)
00108:  * - Busca N docs ASC e pega o primeiro que vira ISO válido
00109:  * - Busca N docs DESC e pega o primeiro que vira ISO válido
00110:  * - Inclui desempate por docId (estável)

00112: async function tryBoundsByField(db, lotteryKey, field, scanLimit = 50) {
00113:   const DOC_ID = admin.firestore.FieldPath.documentId();
00114: 
00115:   const base = db.collection("draws").where("lottery_key", "==", lotteryKey);
00116: 
00117:   const ascSnap = await base
00118:     .orderBy(field, "asc")
00119:     .orderBy(DOC_ID, "asc")
00120:     .limit(scanLimit)
00121:     .get();
00122: 
00123:   const descSnap = await base
00124:     .orderBy(field, "desc")
00125:     .orderBy(DOC_ID, "desc")
00126:     .limit(scanLimit)
00127:     .get();
00128: 
00129:   if (ascSnap.empty || descSnap.empty) {
00130:     return {
00131:       ok: false,
00132:       source: `where(lottery_key)+orderBy(${field})+docId(scan=${scanLimit})`,
00133:     };
00134:   }
00135: 
00136:   function pickFirstValidYmdFromDocs(docs, fieldName) {
00137:     for (const doc of docs || []) {
00138:       const v = doc?.data()?.[fieldName];
00139:       const y = normalizeToYMD(v);
00140:       if (y && isISODate(y)) {
00141:         return { ymd: y, docId: doc.id, raw: v };
00142:       }
00143:     }
00144:     return { ymd: null, docId: null, raw: null };
00145:   }
00146: 
00147:   const minPick = pickFirstValidYmdFromDocs(ascSnap.docs, field);
00148:   const maxPick = pickFirstValidYmdFromDocs(descSnap.docs, field);
00149: 
00150:   return {
00151:     ok: !!(minPick.ymd && maxPick.ymd),
00152:     minYmd: minPick.ymd,
00153:     maxYmd: maxPick.ymd,
00154:     source: `where(lottery_key)+orderBy(${field})+docId(scan=${scanLimit})`,
00155:     minDocId: minPick.docId,
00156:     maxDocId: maxPick.docId,
00157:     minRaw: minPick.raw ?? null,
00158:     maxRaw: maxPick.raw ?? null,
00159:   };
00160: }
00161: 
00162: async function fallbackBoundsByDocIdEdges(db, lotteryKey, edgeLimit) {
00163:   const DOC_ID = admin.firestore.FieldPath.documentId();
00164:   const base = db.collection("draws").where("lottery_key", "==", lotteryKey);
00165: 
00166:   const ascSnap = await base.orderBy(DOC_ID, "asc").limit(edgeLimit).get();
00167:   const descSnap = await base.orderBy(DOC_ID, "asc").limitToLast(edgeLimit).get();
00168: 
00169:   const merged = [...ascSnap.docs, ...descSnap.docs];
00170:   const { minYmd, maxYmd } = pickMinMaxFromDocs(merged);
00171: 
00172:   return {
00173:     ok: !!(minYmd && maxYmd),
00174:     minYmd: minYmd || null,
00175:     maxYmd: maxYmd || null,
00176:     source: `fallback_edges_docId(limit=${edgeLimit})`,
00177:     sampleCount: merged.length,
00178:     firstDocId: ascSnap.docs[0]?.id || null,
00179:     lastDocId: descSnap.docs[descSnap.docs.length - 1]?.id || null,
00180:   };
00181: }

00087:   for (const doc of docs || []) {
00088:     const isSnap = doc && typeof doc.data === "function";
00089:     const d = isSnap ? doc.data() : (doc || {});

00174:     minDocId: minPick.docId,
00175:     maxDocId: maxPick.docId,
00176:     minRaw: minPick.raw ?? null,
00177:     maxRaw: maxPick.raw ?? null,
00178:     source: `${lotteryKey ? "where(lottery_key)+" : ""}orderBy(${fieldName})+docId(scan=${scanLimit})`,
00179:   };
00180: }
00181: 
00182: async function fallbackBoundsByDocIdEdges(db, edgeLimit, { lotteryKey = null } = {}) {
00183:   const DOC_ID = admin.firestore.FieldPath.documentId();
00184: 
00185:   let base = db.collection("draws");
00186:   if (lotteryKey) {
00187:     base = base.where(
00188:       "lottery_key",
00189:       "==",
00190:       String(lotteryKey).trim().toUpperCase()
00191:     );
00192:   }
00193: 
00194:   const ascSnap = await base.orderBy(DOC_ID, "asc").limit(edgeLimit).get();
00195:   const descSnap = await base.orderBy(DOC_ID, "asc").limitToLast(edgeLimit).get();
00196: 
00197:   const merged = [...ascSnap.docs, ...descSnap.docs];
00198: 
00199:   // tenta preferir ymd (se existir), senão date
00200:   let r = pickMinMaxFromDocs(merged, "ymd");
00201:   if (!r.minYmd || !r.maxYmd) r = pickMinMaxFromDocs(merged, "date");
00202: 
00203:   return {
00204:     ok: !!(r.minYmd && r.maxYmd),
00205:     minYmd: r.minYmd || null,
00206:     maxYmd: r.maxYmd || null,
00207:     sampleCount: merged.length,
00208:     firstDocId: ascSnap.docs[0]?.id || null,
00209:     lastDocId: descSnap.docs[descSnap.docs.length - 1]?.id || null,
00210:     source: `${lotteryKey ? "where(lottery_key)+" : ""}fallback_edges_docId(limit=${edgeLimit})`,
00211:   };
00212: }
00213: 
00214: async function main() {
00215:   const db = getDb();
00216: 
00217:   // argv:
00218:   // node script.js PT_RIO 800 50
00219:   const lotteryArg = process.argv[2];
00220:   const lotteryKey = lotteryArg ? String(lotteryArg).trim().toUpperCase() : null;
00221: 
00222:   const edgeArg = Number(process.argv[3]);
00223:   const edgeLimit = Number.isFinite(edgeArg) ? Math.max(200, edgeArg) : 800;
00224: 
00225:   const scanArg = Number(process.argv[4]);
00226:   const scanLimit = Number.isFinite(scanArg) ? Math.max(10, scanArg) : 50;
00227: 
00228:   let source = null;
00229:   let note = null;
00230:   let minYmd = null;
00231:   let maxYmd = null;
00232:   let minDocId = null;
00233:   let maxDocId = null;
00234:   let sampleCount = null;
00235:   let firstDocId = null;
00236:   let lastDocId = null;
00237:

00239:   try {
00240:     const r1 = await tryMinMaxByField(db, "ymd", { lotteryKey, scanLimit });

00243:       minYmd = r1.minYmd;
00244:       maxYmd = r1.maxYmd;
00245:       minDocId = r1.minDocId;
00246:       maxDocId = r1.maxDocId;
00247:     }
00248:   } catch (e) {
00249:     if (isIndexError(e)) {
00250:       note = "faltou índice/condição para orderBy(ymd) (ok, vai para fallback).";
00251:     } else {
00252:       note = note || String(e?.message || e);
00253:     }
00254:   }
00255: 
00256:   // 2) tenta date
00257:   if (!minYmd || !maxYmd) {

00259:       const r2 = await tryMinMaxByField(db, "date", { lotteryKey, scanLimit });
00260:       if (r2.ok) {

00263:         maxYmd = r2.maxYmd;

00265:         maxDocId = r2.maxDocId;
00266:       } else {
00267:         note = note || "campo date/ymd não está consistente como ISO (vai para fallback).";
00268:       }
00269:     } catch (e) {
00270:       if (isIndexError(e)) {
00271:         note = note || "faltou índice/condição para orderBy(date) (vai para fallback).";
00272:       } else {
00273:         note = note || String(e?.message || e);
00274:       }
00275:     }
00276:   }
00277: 
00278:   // 3) fallback definitivo (bordas por docId)
00279:   if (!minYmd || !maxYmd) {
00280:     const fb = await fallbackBoundsByDocIdEdges(db, edgeLimit, { lotteryKey });

00282:     minYmd = fb.minYmd;

00292:   if (note) console.log(`note: ${note}`);
00293:   console.log(`minDate: ${minYmd || "N/A"}`);
00294:   console.log(`maxDate: ${maxYmd || "N/A"}`);

00005: function pad2(n) { return String(n).padStart(2, "0"); }
00006: 
00007: /**
00008:  * Formata Date -> YYYY-MM-DD no timezone informado (padrão: America/Sao_Paulo).
00009:  * Evita “virada de dia” quando o servidor roda em UTC mas o dado é local (BRT).
00010:  */
00011: function formatDateToYMDInTZ(date, timeZone = "America/Sao_Paulo") {
00012:   if (!(date instanceof Date) || Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return null;
00013: 
00014:   // formatToParts => { year, month, day } no TZ pedido
00015:   const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", {
00016:     timeZone,
00017:     year: "numeric",
00018:     month: "2-digit",
00019:     day: "2-digit",
00020:   }).formatToParts(date);
00021: 
00022:   const map = Object.fromEntries(parts.map((p) => [p.type, p.value]));
00023:   const y = map.year, m = map.month, d = map.day;
00024:   if (!y || !m || !d) return null;
00025:   return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
00026: }
00027: 
00028: function isISODateStrict(s) {
00029:   const str = String(s || "").trim();
00030:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(str)) return false;
00031:   const [y, m, d] = str.split("-").map(Number);
00032:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
00033:   return dt.getUTCFullYear() === y && dt.getUTCMonth() === m - 1 && dt.getUTCDate() === d;
00034: }
00035: 
00036: function normalizeToYMD(input, timeZone = "America/Sao_Paulo") {
00037:   if (!input) return null;
00038: 
00039:   // Firestore Timestamp (admin.firestore.Timestamp)
00040:   if (typeof input === "object" && typeof input.toDate === "function") {
00041:     const d = input.toDate();
00042:     const ymd = formatDateToYMDInTZ(d, timeZone);
00043:     if (ymd) return ymd;
00044:   }
00045: 
00046:   // objetos {seconds} / {_seconds}
00047:   if (
00048:     typeof input === "object" &&
00049:     (Number.isFinite(Number(input.seconds)) || Number.isFinite(Number(input._seconds)))
00050:   ) {
00051:     const sec = Number.isFinite(Number(input.seconds)) ? Number(input.seconds) : Number(input._seconds);
00052:     const d = new Date(sec * 1000);
00053:     const ymd = formatDateToYMDInTZ(d, timeZone);
00054:     if (ymd) return ymd;
00055:   }
00056: 
00057:   // Date
00058:   if (input instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(input.getTime())) {
00059:     const ymd = formatDateToYMDInTZ(input, timeZone);
00060:     if (ymd) return ymd;
00061:   }
00062: 
00063:   // strings
00064:   const s = String(input || "").trim();
00065:   if (!s) return null;
00066: 
00067:   // ISO com ou sem hora: pega só YYYY-MM-DD do começo
00068:   const iso = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/);
00069:   if (iso) return `${iso[1]}-${iso[2]}-${iso[3]}`;
00070: 
00071:   // BR: DD/MM/YYYY
00072:   const br = s.match(/^(\d{2})\/(\d{2})\/(\d{4})$/);
00073:   if (br) return `${br[3]}-${br[2]}-${br[1]}`;
00074: 
00075:   return null;
00076: }
00077: 
00078: function normalizeHourLike(v) {
00079:   if (v == null) return null;
00080:   const s = String(v).trim();
00081:   if (!s) return null;
00082:   const m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:[:hH]?(\d{2}))?/);
00083:   if (!m) return s;
00084:   const hh = String(m[1]).padStart(2, "0");
00085:   const mm = String(m[2] || "00").padStart(2, "0");
00086:   return `${hh}:${mm}`;
00087: }
00088: 
00089: function isIndexError(err) {
00090:   const msg = String(err?.message || "").toLowerCase();
00091:   const code = String(err?.code || "").toLowerCase();
00092:   return (
00093:     code.includes("failed-precondition") ||
00094:     msg.includes("failed_precondition") ||
00095:     (msg.includes("index") && msg.includes("create")) ||
00096:     (msg.includes("requires") && msg.includes("index"))
00097:   );
00098: }
00099: 
00100: function extractYmdAndHour(docData) {
00101:   const y =
00102:     docData?.ymd ||
00103:     normalizeToYMD(
00104:       docData?.date ??
00105:       docData?.draw_date ??
00106:       docData?.close_date ??
00107:       docData?.dt ??
00108:       docData?.data
00109:     );
00110: 
00111:   const h = normalizeHourLike(
00112:     docData?.close_hour ??
00113:     docData?.closeHour ??
00114:     docData?.close_hour_raw ??
00115:     docData?.hour ??
00116:     docData?.hora
00117:   );
00118: 
00119:   const ymd = y && isISODateStrict(y) ? y : null;
00120:   const hour = h || null;
00121:   return { ymd, hour };
00122: }
00123: 
00124: function auditCountsFromDocs(rows) {
00125:   const setYmd = new Set();
00126:   const setYmdHour = new Set();
00127:   const mapKeyCount = new Map();
00128:   let invalidYmd = 0;
00129: 
00130:   for (const r of rows) {
00131:     const { ymd, hour } = extractYmdAndHour(r);
00132:     if (!ymd) { invalidYmd++; continue; }
00133:     setYmd.add(ymd);
00134: 
00135:     const key = `${ymd}__${hour || ""}`;
00136:     mapKeyCount.set(key, (mapKeyCount.get(key) || 0) + 1);
00137:     if (hour) setYmdHour.add(key);
00138:   }
00139: 
00140:   const dupKeys = [...mapKeyCount.entries()]
00141:     .filter(([, n]) => n > 1)
00142:     .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]);
00143: 
00144:   return {
00145:     docs_brutos: rows.length,
00146:     unique_ymd: setYmd.size,
00147:     unique_ymd_hour: setYmdHour.size,
00148:     invalid_ymd: invalidYmd,
00149:     keys_total: mapKeyCount.size,
00150:     keys_duplicadas: dupKeys.length,
00151:     top_duplicadas: dupKeys.slice(0, 15),
00152:   };
00153: }
