00155: async function fetchRowsForRange(db, { lotteryKey, fromYmd, toYmd, pageSize = 2000, maxPages = 80 } = {}) {
00156:   const lot = lotteryKey ? String(lotteryKey).trim().toUpperCase() : null;
00157: 
00158:   // 1) tentativa indexada (aceita range parcial)
00159:   try {
00160:     let q = db.collection("draws");
00161:     if (lot) q = q.where("lottery_key", "==", lot);
00162:     if (fromYmd) q = q.where("ymd", ">=", fromYmd);
00163:     if (toYmd) q = q.where("ymd", "<=", toYmd);
00164: 
00165:     const snap = await q.get();
00166:     const rows = snap.docs.map((d) => ({ id: d.id, ...(d.data() || {}) }));
00167:     return { rows, source: "query: where(lottery_key)+where(ymd>=/<=)", usedFallback: false };
00168:   } catch (e) {
00169:     if (!isIndexError(e)) throw e;
00170:   }
00171: 
00172:   // 2) fallback sem índice: pagina por docId e filtra em memória
00173:   const DOC_ID = admin.firestore.FieldPath.documentId();
00174:   let q = db.collection("draws");
00175:   if (lot) q = q.where("lottery_key", "==", lot);
00176:   q = q.orderBy(DOC_ID, "asc");
00177: 
00178:   let last = null;
00179:   const out = [];
00180: 
00181:   for (let page = 1; page <= maxPages; page++) {
00182:     let qq = q.limit(pageSize);
00183:     if (last) qq = qq.startAfter(last);
00184: 
00185:     const snap = await qq.get();
00186:     if (snap.empty) break;
00187: 
00188:     for (const doc of snap.docs) {
00189:       const data = doc.data() || {};
00190:       const ymd = normalizeToYMD(data.ymd ?? data.date ?? data.draw_date ?? data.close_date ?? data.dt ?? data.data);
00191:       if (!ymd || !isISODateStrict(ymd)) continue;
00192:       if (fromYmd && ymd < fromYmd) continue;
00193:       if (toYmd && ymd > toYmd) continue;
00194:       out.push({ id: doc.id, ...data });
00195:     }
00196: 
00197:     last = snap.docs[snap.docs.length - 1];
00198:   }
00199: 
00200:   return { rows: out, source: "fallback: paginate by docId + filter(ymd)", usedFallback: true };
00201: }
00202: 
00203: function parseArg(name, def = null) {
00204:   const i = process.argv.findIndex((x) => x === name);
00205:   if (i >= 0 && process.argv[i + 1] != null) return process.argv[i + 1];
00206:   return def;
00207: }

00209: async function main() {
00210:   const db = getDb();
00211: 
00212:   const lot = parseArg("--lot", "PT_RIO");
00213:   const fromArg = parseArg("--from", null);

00222:   const toYmd = toArg && isISODateStrict(toArg) ? toArg : null;

00126:                             se agenda existir e não bater (sem range para a data), ERRA (ao invés de cair no fallback)
00127:   --gapsFile=PATH
00128:                             arquivo de gaps da fonte (API_NO_SLOT). default:
00129:                             backend/data/source_gaps/<LOTTERY>.json
00130:   --noDrawFile=PATH
00131:                             arquivo de dias sem sorteio (holiday_no_draw/blocked). default:
00132:                             backend/data/no_draw_days/<LOTTERY>.json
00133:   --today=YYYY-MM-DD
00134:                             força qual "dia atual" considerar (debug/replay). default: hoje (local)
00135:   --nowHM=HH:mm
00136:                             força o "agora" (minutos) para o cap do dia atual.
00137:                             alternativa: variável de ambiente NOW_HM=HH:mm
00138:   --slotGraceMin=MIN
00139:                             tolerância (minutos) após a hora do slot para considerar "já publicado". default: 25
00140:   --noCapToday
00141:                             desativa o cap do dia atual (volta a esperar todos os slots do schedule)
00142: 
00143: Observação:
00144:   ✅ endYmd é opcional:
00145:     - se omitido, usa "today" (ou --today)
00146: 
00147: Saída:
00148:   backend/logs/auditSlots-PT_RIO-<start>_to_<end>.json
00149: `.trim()
00150:   );
00151: }

00153: // =====================
00154: // Fallback hardcoded (compat) — PT_RIO
00155: // =====================
00156: function shouldInclude09ForDate(ymd, include09FromYmd) {
00157:   if (include09FromYmd && isISODate(include09FromYmd)) {
00158:     return ymd >= include09FromYmd;
00159:   }
00160:   return false;
00161: }
00162: 
00163: /**
00164:  * Retorna:
00165:  *  - expectedHard: horas que DEVEM existir (buraco real se faltar)
00166:  *  - expectedSoft: horas opcionais/variáveis
00167:  */
00168: function expectedHoursPT_RIO_FALLBACK(ymd, dow, include09FromYmd, soft18WedSat) {
00169:   const has09 = shouldInclude09ForDate(ymd, include09FromYmd);
00170: 
00171:   const hardWeek = ["11", "14", "16", "18", "21"];
00172:   const hardSun = ["11", "14", "16"];
00173: 
00174:   const expectedHard = [];
00175:   const expectedSoft = [];
00176: 
00177:   if (dow === 0) {
00178:     expectedHard.push(...hardSun);
00179:     if (has09) expectedSoft.push("09"); // domingo 09 variável
00180:     return { expectedHard, expectedSoft, mode: "fallback" };
00181:   }
